STEP5 consistency review (prompt P8). The implementation has landed. `apriori verify --change gate-degrades` is GREEN, so the MECHANICAL binding is already proven — every scenario has a passing test carrying its ID, no orphans within the change scope. Your job is what binding CANNOT prove.

[Input]
* SPEC-DOC (the delta): apriori/changes/gate-degrades/specs/  (gate/, doctor/, spec-runner/)

* Ledger: apriori/changes/gate-degrades/review/issues.md (all rows terminal; new findings take IMPL-n IDs)

[Dimensions]
1. Semantic faithfulness: for EACH scenario (GT-30..GT-38, the modified GT-11 and DR-07, SR-73..SR-75), does its test actually assert the behaviour the scenario describes, or merely share its ID while asserting something weaker? A green test can be empty.
2. Behaviour the spec requires but the code does not implement, that a bound test failed to catch.
3. continue/skip/silently-ignored branches — does the spec require them to be user-visible.
4. Where external input or permissions are touched: unvalidated input, missing authz, secrets in logs.
5. Guarantee claims: every "always / never / zero times / byte-identical / exactly" phrase in the spec, KB or CHANGELOG must have a test that injects the adversarial condition and observes it hold. In particular:
   * "a configured path is byte-identical to its pre-seam form" — is that actually exercised, or merely asserted in prose?
   * "the matcher child is spawned ZERO times" / "the projection builder is invoked ZERO times" / "no test process is spawned" — three different guarantees needing three different seams. Are they each really bound?
   * "`blocked` counts blocked only" — proven?
6. The requirement's §3.1 seven-row test-command classification and §3.2 eight-row matrix: walk each row against the implementation in lib/gate.js and say whether the code actually produces the stated outcome. Name any row that is unreachable, mis-ordered, or produces a different result than the matrix says.

[Also worth checking]
* The producer self-caught one false binding during STEP5: a test named `GT-15a …` whose leading ID would have been parsed as the unrelated store scenario `GT-15`. It was renamed `SR-73b` and SR-73's THEN was extended. Are there any OTHER test names in the change whose leading token binds to a scenario they do not actually exercise?
* `npm run verify` (store-only form) reports 12 ORPHANs — the change's own new IDs, not yet merged into the store. Confirm that is the expected in-flight state and not a real gap.

[Scope] Count toward the verdict only spec-vs-code gaps. Style and taste — advisory. If you run tests in your read-only sandbox, treat degraded output as a sandbox artifact, not a finding.

@@ -2,6 +2,40 @@
 
 All notable changes to `apriori-cli`. Versions follow semver; the stability promise: CLI surface & flags, `--json` shapes, the delta format and the flow-state schema only break in a major.
 
+## Unreleased · gate degrades the check it cannot run — `INCOMPLETE`, exit code 3
+
+**Exit-code semantics extended.** `apriori gate` gains a fourth outcome: `GATE: INCOMPLETE`
+with **exit code 3**, and `checks[].status` gains `skipped`. If your CI treats any non-zero
+exit as failure, an INCOMPLETE gate will now fail the job — that is the intended reading: a
+gate that could not run its binding check has not passed. The `--json` key set is unchanged
+(`{change, stage, checks, result, blocked, errors}` — still no `code` field); only the value
+domains of `result` and `checks[].status` grew, and `blocked` still counts blocked checks
+only, never skipped ones.
+
+**Why.** A project whose `apriori/process-config.md` lacked a `test-cmd` row got exit 2 from
+`apriori gate` before a single check ran — so C2 (tasks all checked) and C4 (ledger terminal)
+never executed either, though neither needs a test command. In a real brownfield project that
+meant the gate ran zero times across an entire change's life, while 45 unchecked tasks and
+zero consistency reviews sailed through. Now a missing test command skips C1 alone: the other
+six checks run and report real conclusions, and the aggregate says so. A *broken* test-command
+source (conflicting or unreadable config, an empty or whitespace-only `--test-cmd`, a
+non-string value through the API) stays exit 2 — broken is not absent. `--test-cmd` is now
+judged by PRESENCE, matching `--id-pattern`: an empty flag is an error, never a config
+fallback. A confirmed block still outranks a skip.
+
+**`apriori doctor` stops calling that project healthy.** D5 reported `n/a` when no test
+command was configured, and `n/a` is not a finding — so doctor printed `DOCTOR: HEALTHY` about
+a project whose gate was dead. It is now a finding naming the consequence, with the
+`process-config.md` fix. An explicit `--no-run` is still `n/a`: a deliberate skip is not a
+defect, and that branch now sorts ahead of the missing-command one.
+
+**Internals.** `lib/spec-runner.js` exports the change-projection builder plus a call-time
+resolver and two test-only seams, so gate's skipped-C1 path provably shares verify's one
+projection implementation rather than growing a second — a divergence there would have
+silently un-blocked C7.
+
+391 tests.
+

+// The same shape as checkBinding, for the path where no test command exists. C1 cannot run,
+// but everything C1 was ALSO producing for its neighbours still can: C7 needs the projection,
+// and a broken id-pattern is still a broken config. Nothing here spawns a process.
+function checkBindingSkipped(cwd, name, stage, idPattern) {
+  // a broken pattern is a broken CONFIG, not an absent one — it stays an evaluation error
+  // (GT-24 keeps holding). Compile-checking is in-process; no scenario is ever MATCHED here,
+  // so no matcher child is spawned and GT-25's runtime precondition cannot arise.
+  const idp = resolveIdPattern(cwd, idPattern);
+  if (idp.error) return { infra: [idp.error] };
+  const detail = 'skipped — no test command (pass --test-cmd or add a test-cmd row to '
+    + 'apriori/process-config.md); the binding check did not run';
+  const c1 = { id: 'C1', status: 'skipped', detail };
+  // archived: the deltas are already merged and C7 is n/a — building a projection could only
+  // manufacture a false block, so it is never built at all.
+  if (stage === 'archived') return { check: c1, projection: null };
+  const b = sr.currentProjectionBuilder()(name, cwd);
+  if (b.errors && b.errors.length) return { infra: b.errors };
+  // fail closed: no trustworthy texts is untrustworthy even when nothing said why. Reporting
+  // it with an empty errors list would be an ERROR that cannot explain itself.
+  if (!b.texts) return { infra: ['projection produced no trustworthy texts and reported no error — refusing to judge'] };
+  return { check: c1, projection: b.projection };
+}
+
+// The test command has three fates, and "broken" is not "absent". A flag that is present but
+// empty (or all whitespace) is an operator error in THIS invocation and must never fall back
+// to the config; an empty config VALUE, by contrast, was already normalised to "no such row"
+// by the shared reader (config.js), so gate sees an absence and treats it as one.
+function resolveTestCmd(opts, cwd) {
+  const flag = opts.testCmd;
+  if (flag !== undefined && flag !== null) {
+    if (typeof flag !== 'string') return { kind: 'error', error: `--test-cmd must be a string (got ${Array.isArray(flag) ? 'array' : typeof flag})` };
+    if (flag.trim() === '') return { kind: 'error', error: 'empty --test-cmd — pass a command or omit the flag' };
+    return { kind: 'ok', value: flag };
+  }
+  const c = configTestCmd(cwd);
+  if (c && c.error) return { kind: 'error', error: `verify: ${c.error}` };
+  if (c) return { kind: 'ok', value: c };
+  return { kind: 'absent' };
+}
+

+// deliberately NOT exported — only verify() reads it; gate never runs a test command
+function currentTestRunner() { return testRunnerOverride || runTestCommand; }

    19	function mkProject(files) {
    20	  const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'apriori-degrade-'));
    21	  for (const [rel, content] of Object.entries(files)) {
    22	    const p = path.join(root, rel);
    23	    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(p), { recursive: true });
    24	    fs.writeFileSync(p, content);
    25	  }
    26	  return root;
    27	}

   270	test('SR-73 both consumers pass through the same replaceable builder', () => {
   271	  const root = noCmdProject();

   274	  let viaA;
   275	  try {
   276	    // ① gate module already required above, BEFORE any override existed
   277	    sr._setProjectionBuilder(A);
   278	    const g = gate.runGate({ cwd: root, change: 'c' });

   281	    assert.deepStrictEqual(v.errors, [], 'the configured path still works');
   282	    viaA = seen.slice();
   283	    assert.deepStrictEqual(viaA, ['A', 'A'], 'gate and verify both went through the SAME wrapper');

   287	      ['clean', DELTA],
   288	      ['unstamped mutation', '## MODIFIED Requirements\n\n### Requirement: Alpha\n\n#### Scenario: XA-01 base\n- t2\n'],
   289	      ['malformed', '### Requirement: Beta\n\n#### Scenario: XB-01 x\n- t\n'],
   290	      ['diverged CAS', '<!-- apriori-base: sha256:' + 'a'.repeat(64) + ' -->\n\n## MODIFIED Requirements\n\n### Requirement: Alpha\n\n#### Scenario: XA-01 base\n- t2\n'],
   291	      ['conflict', '## ADDED Requirements\n\n### Requirement: Alpha\n\n#### Scenario: XA-01 base\n- different body\n'],
   292	    ];
   293	    for (const [label, delta] of fixtures) {
   294	      const p = noCmdProject('c', { 'apriori/changes/c/specs/kv/spec.md': delta });
   295	      const captured = [];

   297	      gate.runGate({ cwd: p, change: 'c' });
   298	      sr.verify({ change: 'c', cwd: p, testCmd: 'node -e "console.log(1)"' });
   299	      assert.strictEqual(captured.length, 2, `${label}: both paths built a projection`);
   300	      assert.deepStrictEqual(captured[0].projection, captured[1].projection, `${label}: full projection equal`);
   301	      assert.deepStrictEqual(captured[0].errors, captured[1].errors, `${label}: errors equal`);
   302	    }
   303	    // the sixth fixture — no delta files at all
   304	    const bare = mkProject({
   305	      'apriori/specs/kv/spec.md': STORE,
   306	      'apriori/changes/c/flow-state.md': FLOW('c'),
   307	      'apriori/changes/c/tasks.md': '- [x] T1\n',
   308	      'apriori/changes/c/review/issues.md': LEDGER_OK,
   309	    });
   310	    const cap2 = [];

   312	    gate.runGate({ cwd: bare, change: 'c' });
   313	    sr.verify({ change: 'c', cwd: bare, testCmd: 'node -e "console.log(1)"' });

   315	    assert.deepStrictEqual(cap2[0].errors, cap2[1].errors);
   316	
   317	    // ③ only NOW swap the reference — proving it is resolved at CALL time, not module load
   318	    let bCalls = 0;
   319	    sr._setProjectionBuilder((...a) => { bCalls++; return sr.buildChangeProjection(...a); });
   320	    gate.runGate({ cwd: root, change: 'c' });
   321	    assert.strictEqual(bCalls, 1, 'gate read the NEW reference — nothing was frozen at module load');
   322	  } finally { sr._setProjectionBuilder(null); }
   323	});

     1	'use strict';
     2	/*
     3	 * apriori gate — aggregate the MECHANICAL exit conditions for one change into one exit code.
     4	 * 0 = PASS · 1 = BLOCKED · 2 = the evaluation itself is untrustworthy.
     5	 * Strictly read-only. PASS covers machine checks only — human gates remain human.
     6	 */
     7	const fs = require('fs');
     8	const path = require('path');
     9	const { spawnSync } = require('child_process');
    10	const { parseFlowState, parseLedger } = require('./status');
    11	const sr = require('./spec-runner');
    12	const { verify, configTestCmd } = sr;
    13	const { CHANGE_NAME_RE, containsReal } = require('./archive-merge');
    14	const { resolveChange, configCas, configCasProblem } = require('./resolve');
    15	const { resolveIdPattern } = require('./config');
    16	const { withStrict } = require('./args');
    17	
    18	const STEP_ENUM = ['STEP0', 'STEP1', 'STEP2', 'STEP3', 'STEP4', 'STEP5', 'STEP6',
    19	  'INTENT-CARD', 'SPIKE', 'EXTRACTION', 'DONE', 'ABANDONED'];
    20	const TIER_ENUM = ['trivial', 'medium', 'large'];
    21	const CAVEAT = 'mechanical checks only; human gates remain human';
    22	
    23	function err(res, msg) { res.errors.push(msg); res.result = 'ERROR'; res.code = 2; return res; }
    24	
    25	
    26	// --- individual checks; each returns {id, status: 'pass'|'blocked'|'n/a', detail} ---
    27	
    28	function checkFlowState(state, name) {
    29	  for (const key of ['change', 'tier', 'track', 'lineage', 'current-step']) {
    30	    const v = state[key];
    31	    if (v === undefined || v === '') return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: required key '${key}' missing` };
    32	    if (v.includes('<') || v.includes('>')) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: '${key}' is an unfilled placeholder (${v})` };
    33	  }
    34	  if (state.change !== name) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: 'change' is '${state.change}', expected '${name}'` };
    35	  if (!STEP_ENUM.includes(state['current-step'])) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: 'current-step' '${state['current-step']}' not in the legal vocabulary` };
    36	  if (!TIER_ENUM.includes(state.tier)) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: 'tier' '${state.tier}' not in {trivial, medium, large}` };
    37	  return { id: 'C3', status: 'pass', detail: `legal (tier ${state.tier}, ${state['current-step']})` };
    38	}
    39	
    40	function checkTasks(dir, tier) {
    41	  const p = path.join(dir, 'tasks.md');
    42	  if (!fs.existsSync(p)) {
    43	    return tier === 'trivial'
    44	      ? { id: 'C2', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no tasks.md — trivial tier has no STEP2' }
    45	      : { id: 'C2', status: 'blocked', detail: `tasks.md missing at ${p}` };
    46	  }
    47	  const open = (fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8').match(/^\s*-\s\[\s\]/gm) || []).length;
    48	  return open
    49	    ? { id: 'C2', status: 'blocked', detail: `tasks.md has ${open} unchecked box(es)` }
    50	    : { id: 'C2', status: 'pass', detail: 'all tasks checked' };
    51	}
    52	
    53	// the ledger vocabulary, one classifier shared by C4 and the corpus test (GT-15).
    54	// leading token, case-insensitive; rejected-verified BEFORE rejected (alternation order).
    55	const STATUS_RE = /^(open|fixed|verified|rejected-verified|rejected|advisory-acked|waived)\b/i;
    56	function classifyStatus(status) {
    57	  const m = STATUS_RE.exec(status);
    58	  if (!m) return { legal: false, terminal: false, needsReason: false, hasReason: false, isWaived: false };
    59	  const tok = m[1].toLowerCase();
    60	  const needsReason = tok === 'rejected' || tok === 'rejected-verified' || tok === 'waived';
    61	  return {
    62	    legal: true,
    63	    terminal: tok === 'verified' || tok === 'rejected-verified' || tok === 'waived' || tok === 'advisory-acked',
    64	    needsReason,
    65	    hasReason: !needsReason || /\w/.test(status.slice(m[0].length)),
    66	    isWaived: tok === 'waived',
    67	    token: tok,
    68	  };
    69	}
    70	
    71	const escapeRe = (s) => s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
    72	
    73	// gates: entries from a flow-state text — the block runs from the unindented `gates:` line
    74	// to the next unindented top-level key; entries start `- `, continuation lines attach.
    75	function gatesEntries(flowText) {
    76	  const start = flowText.search(/^gates:/m);
    77	  if (start < 0) return [];
    78	  const rest = flowText.slice(start + 'gates:'.length);
    79	  const end = rest.search(/^[A-Za-z][\w-]*:/m);
    80	  const block = end < 0 ? rest : rest.slice(0, end);
    81	  const entries = [];
    82	  for (const line of block.split('\n')) {
    83	    if (/^\s*-\s/.test(line)) entries.push(line.trim());
    84	    else if (entries.length && line.trim()) entries[entries.length - 1] += ' ' + line.trim();
    85	  }
    86	  return entries;
    87	}
    88	
    89	// a waive is a HUMAN act: one single gates: entry must carry the row ID as an exact
    90	// token (LS-1 never matches inside LS-10) AND the word waive/waived.
    91	function waiveEvidence(flowText, id) {
    92	  const idRe = new RegExp(`(^|[^A-Za-z0-9-])${escapeRe(id)}([^A-Za-z0-9-]|$)`);
    93	  return gatesEntries(flowText).some((e) => idRe.test(e) && /waiv/i.test(e));
    94	}
    95	
    96	function checkLedger(tier, stage, dir) {
    97	  const p = path.join(dir, 'review', 'issues.md');
    98	  if (!fs.existsSync(p)) {
    99	    return tier === 'trivial'
   100	      ? { id: 'C4', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no ledger — trivial tier may never open one' }
   101	      : { id: 'C4', status: 'blocked', detail: `ledger missing at ${p}` };
   102	  }
   103	  const rows = parseLedger(fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8'));
   104	  const fsPath = dir ? path.join(dir, 'flow-state.md') : null;
   105	  const flowText = fsPath && fs.existsSync(fsPath) ? fs.readFileSync(fsPath, 'utf8') : '';
   106	  const bad = [];
   107	  for (const r of rows) {
   108	    const c = classifyStatus(r.status);
   109	    if (!c.legal) { bad.push(`${r.id}: '${r.status}' is not in the ledger vocabulary`); continue; }
   110	    if (c.needsReason && !c.hasReason) { bad.push(`${r.id} is ${c.token} without a reason`); continue; }
   111	    if (c.isWaived && !waiveEvidence(flowText, r.id)) {
   112	      bad.push(`${r.id} is waived without a gates: entry recording the human decision`); continue;
   113	    }
   114	    if (c.token === 'open') { bad.push(`${r.id} is open`); continue; }
   115	    if (stage === 'archived' && !c.terminal) {
   116	      bad.push(c.token === 'fixed'
   117	        ? `${r.id} is fixed but never verified — reviewer must verify, or the human waives it`
   118	        : `${r.id} is rejected without reviewer concurrence — flip to rejected-verified, or the human waives it`);
   119	    }
   120	  }
   121	  return bad.length
   122	    ? { id: 'C4', status: 'blocked', detail: bad.join('; ') }
   123	    : { id: 'C4', status: 'pass', detail: `${rows.length} row(s), none blocking` };
   124	}
   125	
   126	// the bundle review/ dir is the shared evidence root for C4 and C5 — when present it must be
   127	// a REAL contained directory (symlinked/escaping/non-dir entries block, never read through)
   128	function reviewDirDefect(dir) {
   129	  const rd = path.join(dir, 'review');
   130	  let st;
   131	  try { st = fs.lstatSync(rd); } catch { return null; }  // lstat, not exists: a dangling symlink is a defect, not absence
   132	  if (st.isSymbolicLink()) return `review is a symlink: ${rd}`;
   133	  if (!st.isDirectory()) return `review is not a directory: ${rd}`;
   134	  if (!containsReal(dir, rd)) return `review escapes the change dir: ${rd}`;
   135	  return null;
   136	}
   137	
   138	function checkEvidence(dir) {
   139	  const rd = path.join(dir, 'review');
   140	  const docs = [];
   141	  if (fs.existsSync(rd)) {
   142	    for (const e of fs.readdirSync(rd, { withFileTypes: true })) {
   143	      if (!e.name.endsWith('.md') || e.name === 'issues.md' || /-raw(\.|$)/.test(e.name.replace(/\.md$/, ''))) continue;
   144	      if (e.name.includes('-raw')) continue;
   145	      const p = path.join(rd, e.name);
   146	      if (fs.lstatSync(p).isSymbolicLink())
   147	        return { id: 'C5', status: 'blocked', detail: `evidence doc is a symlink: ${e.name}` };
   148	      if (!fs.lstatSync(p).isFile()) continue;
   149	      docs.push(p);
   150	    }
   151	  }
   152	  const missing = [];
   153	  let verdictDocs = 0;
   154	  for (const doc of docs) {
   155	    if (!/^VERDICT:/m.test(fs.readFileSync(doc, 'utf8'))) continue;
   156	    verdictDocs++;
   157	    const stem = path.basename(doc).replace(/\.md$/, '');
   158	    const hasRaw = fs.existsSync(rd) && fs.readdirSync(rd).some((n) => {
   159	      if (!n.startsWith(stem + '-raw')) return false;
   160	      const rp = path.join(rd, n);
   161	      return fs.lstatSync(rp).isFile();
   162	    });
   163	    if (!hasRaw) missing.push(path.basename(doc));
   164	  }
   165	  if (missing.length) return { id: 'C5', status: 'blocked', detail: `verdict doc(s) without a raw archive: ${missing.join(', ')}` };
   166	  return { id: 'C5', status: 'pass', detail: `${verdictDocs} review doc(s), every verdict has raw evidence` };
   167	}
   168	
   169	function checkKb(cwd, dir) {
   170	  const specsDir = path.join(dir, 'specs');
   171	  const modules = new Set();
   172	  const walk = (d, base) => {
   173	    if (!fs.existsSync(d)) return;
   174	    for (const e of fs.readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true })) {
   175	      const p = path.join(d, e.name);
   176	      if (e.isDirectory()) walk(p, base);
   177	      else if (e.name.endsWith('.md')) modules.add(path.relative(base, p).split(path.sep)[0]);
   178	    }
   179	  };
   180	  walk(specsDir, specsDir);
   181	  if (!modules.size) return { id: 'C6', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no delta specs to map' };
   182	
   183	  // build the module → truth index (c6-truth-binding): a truth doc binds by its
   184	  // declared store-module (default = basename), never by a filename assumption
   185	  const truthDir = path.join(cwd, 'apriori', 'truth');
   186	  const index = new Map();                 // module → { text, sourceFiles, basename }
   187	  const conflicts = new Map();             // module → "declared by A and B"
   188	  if (fs.existsSync(truthDir)) {
   189	    for (const e of fs.readdirSync(truthDir).sort()) {
   190	      if (!e.endsWith('.md')) continue;
   191	      const text = fs.readFileSync(path.join(truthDir, e), 'utf8');
   192	      const h2 = text.search(/^##\s/m);
   193	      const header = h2 >= 0 ? text.slice(0, h2) : text;
   194	      const smM = header.match(/^store-module:\s*(.+)$/m);
   195	      const sfM = header.match(/^source-files:\s*(.+)$/m);
   196	      const mods = smM ? smM[1].trim().split(/\s+/) : [e.replace(/\.md$/, '')];
   197	      const sourceFiles = sfM ? sfM[1].trim().split(/\s+/) : null;   // null = default lib/<module>.js
   198	      for (const m of mods) {
   199	        if (index.has(m)) conflicts.set(m, `${m}: declared by both ${index.get(m).basename} and ${e}`);
   200	        else index.set(m, { text, sourceFiles, basename: e });
   201	      }
   202	    }
   203	  }
   204	  const stripFencesKb = (t) => t.replace(/```[\s\S]*?```/g, '');
   205	  const blocked = [], notes = [];
   206	  let checked = 0;
   207	  for (const m of [...modules].sort()) {
   208	    if (conflicts.has(m)) { blocked.push(conflicts.get(m)); continue; }
   209	    const entry = index.get(m);
   210	    if (!entry) { notes.push(`${m}: no truth doc`); continue; }
   211	    const noFence = stripFencesKb(entry.text);
   212	    const sc = noFence.match(/^source-commit:\s+(\S+)/m);
   213	    if (!sc) {
   214	      const attempt = noFence.split('\n').some((l) => l.includes('source-commit:') && !/^source-commit:\s+\S+/.test(l));
   215	      notes.push(attempt
   216	        ? `${m}: source-commit is not in the canonical fence-outside line-start form 'source-commit: <ref>'`
   217	        : `${m}: truth doc has no source-commit`);
   218	      continue;
   219	    }
   220	    const ref = sc[1];
   221	    const explicit = entry.sourceFiles !== null;
   222	    const declared = explicit ? entry.sourceFiles : [`lib/${m}.js`];
   223	    // an explicit declaration is a complete promise: every token must be verifiable
   224	    const usable = [];
   225	    let tokenBad = null;
   226	    for (const rel of declared) {
   227	      // an explicit token is a repo-relative promise: reject absolute/backslash/empty/. or .. segments
   228	      if (explicit && (rel === '' || path.isAbsolute(rel) || rel.includes('\\') ||
   229	          rel.split('/').some((seg) => seg === '' || seg === '.' || seg === '..'))) {
   230	        tokenBad = `${m}: malformed source-files token (must be a normalized repo-relative path): ${rel}`; break;
   231	      }
   232	      const abs = path.join(cwd, rel);
   233	      let st = null; try { st = fs.lstatSync(abs); } catch { /* absent */ }
   234	      if (!st) { if (explicit) { tokenBad = `${m}: declared source-files token missing: ${rel}`; break; } continue; }
   235	      if (st.isSymbolicLink()) { if (explicit) { tokenBad = `${m}: declared source-files token is a symlink: ${rel}`; break; } continue; }
   236	      if (!containsReal(cwd, abs)) { if (explicit) { tokenBad = `${m}: declared source-files token escapes the repo: ${rel}`; break; } continue; }
   237	      if (!st.isFile() && !st.isDirectory()) { if (explicit) { tokenBad = `${m}: declared source-files token is neither file nor directory: ${rel}`; break; } continue; }
   238	      usable.push(rel);
   239	    }
   240	    if (tokenBad) { blocked.push(tokenBad); continue; }
   241	    if (!usable.length) { notes.push(`${m}: no ${declared.join(' / ')} to compare`); continue; }
   242	    const g = spawnSync('git', ['-C', cwd, 'log', '--oneline', `${ref}..HEAD`, '--', ...usable], { encoding: 'utf8' });
   243	    if (g.error) { notes.push(`${m}: git unavailable (${g.error.message})`); continue; }
   244	    if (g.status !== 0) { notes.push(`${m}: git failed (${(g.stderr || '').split('\n')[0]})`); continue; }
   245	    const n = g.stdout.trim() ? g.stdout.trim().split('\n').length : 0;
   246	    if (n > 0) blocked.push(`${m}: ${n} commit(s) since ${ref}`);
   247	    else checked++;
   248	  }
   249	  if (blocked.length) return { id: 'C6', status: 'blocked', detail: blocked.join('; ') };
   250	  if (checked > 0) return { id: 'C6', status: 'pass', detail: `${checked} module stamp(s) up to date${notes.length ? `; ${notes.join('; ')}` : ''}` };
   251	  return { id: 'C6', status: 'n/a', detail: notes.join('; ') };
   252	}
   253	
   254	function checkBinding(cwd, name, stage, testCmd, idPattern) {
   255	  const run = stage === 'in-flight'
   256	    ? verify({ change: name, cwd, testCmd, idPattern })
   257	    : verify({ specs: [path.join(cwd, 'apriori', 'specs')], cwd, testCmd, idPattern });
   258	  if (run.errors.length) return { infra: run.errors };
   259	  const v = run.verdict;
   260	  const clean = v.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0;
   261	  // in-flight runs carry the informative store summary (change-scoped verdict, GT-26/27)
   262	  const sr2 = run.storeReport;
   263	  const suffix = sr2
   264	    ? `; store: ${sr2.boundRed.length} red, ${sr2.unbound.length} unbound, ${sr2.orphan.length} orphan, ${sr2.unidentified.length} unidentified, ${sr2.unattributedFailures.count} unattributed, ${sr2.duplicates.length} duplicate(s) outstanding`
   265	    : '';
   266	  const label0 = sr2 ? `${stage}, change-scoped` : stage;
   267	  if (clean) return { check: { id: 'C1', status: 'pass', detail: `verify GREEN (${label0})${suffix}` }, projection: run.projection };
   268	  const parts = [];
   269	  for (const [label, arr] of [['red', v.boundRed], ['unbound', v.unbound], ['orphan', v.orphan], ['unidentified', v.unidentified], ['unattributed-failures', v.unattributed || []], ['duplicate-IDs', run.duplicates]])
   270	    if (arr.length) parts.push(`${arr.length} ${label}`);
   271	  return { check: { id: 'C1', status: 'blocked', detail: `verify GAPS: ${parts.join(', ')}${suffix}` }, projection: run.projection };
   272	}
   273	
   274	
   275	// The same shape as checkBinding, for the path where no test command exists. C1 cannot run,
   276	// but everything C1 was ALSO producing for its neighbours still can: C7 needs the projection,
   277	// and a broken id-pattern is still a broken config. Nothing here spawns a process.
   278	function checkBindingSkipped(cwd, name, stage, idPattern) {
   279	  // a broken pattern is a broken CONFIG, not an absent one — it stays an evaluation error
   280	  // (GT-24 keeps holding). Compile-checking is in-process; no scenario is ever MATCHED here,
   281	  // so no matcher child is spawned and GT-25's runtime precondition cannot arise.
   282	  const idp = resolveIdPattern(cwd, idPattern);
   283	  if (idp.error) return { infra: [idp.error] };
   284	  const detail = 'skipped — no test command (pass --test-cmd or add a test-cmd row to '
   285	    + 'apriori/process-config.md); the binding check did not run';
   286	  const c1 = { id: 'C1', status: 'skipped', detail };
   287	  // archived: the deltas are already merged and C7 is n/a — building a projection could only
   288	  // manufacture a false block, so it is never built at all.
   289	  if (stage === 'archived') return { check: c1, projection: null };
   290	  const b = sr.currentProjectionBuilder()(name, cwd);
   291	  if (b.errors && b.errors.length) return { infra: b.errors };
   292	  // fail closed: no trustworthy texts is untrustworthy even when nothing said why. Reporting
   293	  // it with an empty errors list would be an ERROR that cannot explain itself.
   294	  if (!b.texts) return { infra: ['projection produced no trustworthy texts and reported no error — refusing to judge'] };
   295	  return { check: c1, projection: b.projection };
   296	}
   297	
   298	// The test command has three fates, and "broken" is not "absent". A flag that is present but
   299	// empty (or all whitespace) is an operator error in THIS invocation and must never fall back
   300	// to the config; an empty config VALUE, by contrast, was already normalised to "no such row"
   301	// by the shared reader (config.js), so gate sees an absence and treats it as one.
   302	function resolveTestCmd(opts, cwd) {
   303	  const flag = opts.testCmd;
   304	  if (flag !== undefined && flag !== null) {
   305	    if (typeof flag !== 'string') return { kind: 'error', error: `--test-cmd must be a string (got ${Array.isArray(flag) ? 'array' : typeof flag})` };
   306	    if (flag.trim() === '') return { kind: 'error', error: 'empty --test-cmd — pass a command or omit the flag' };
   307	    return { kind: 'ok', value: flag };
   308	  }
   309	  const c = configTestCmd(cwd);
   310	  if (c && c.error) return { kind: 'error', error: `verify: ${c.error}` };
   311	  if (c) return { kind: 'ok', value: c };
   312	  return { kind: 'absent' };
   313	}
   314	
   315	// C7: unstamped mutation deltas are denied by default — a waiver is always visible (GT-16).
   316	// Archived stage: the deltas are already merged; there is nothing left to stamp-check.
   317	function checkCas(projection, stage, noCas, cwd) {
   318	  if (stage === 'archived') return { id: 'C7', status: 'n/a', detail: 'deltas already merged' };
   319	  const um = (projection && projection.unstampedMutations) || [];
   320	  if (!um.length) return { id: 'C7', status: 'pass', detail: 'every mutation delta is stamped' };  // nothing to consult — silent pass (GT-16)
   321	  if (noCas) return { id: 'C7', status: 'pass', detail: 'waived (--no-cas)' };
   322	  const problem = configCasProblem(cwd);                       // consumption-time only
   323	  if (problem) return { id: 'C7', status: 'blocked', detail: `${problem} — a broken config never equals a waiver` };
   324	  if (configCas(cwd) === 'optional') return { id: 'C7', status: 'pass', detail: 'waived (process-config cas: optional)' };
   325	  return { id: 'C7', status: 'blocked',
   326	    detail: `unstamped mutation delta(s): ${um.join(', ')} — run: apriori stamp <store-file> (or waive with --no-cas / a process-config cas: optional row)` };
   327	}
   328	
   329	// The whole evaluation. → { code, stage, checks, result, blocked, errors }
   330	function runGate(opts) {
   331	  const cwd = opts.cwd || process.cwd();
   332	  const res = { code: 0, stage: null, checks: [], result: 'PASS', blocked: 0, errors: [], change: opts.change || null };
   333	  if (!opts.change) return err(res, USAGE);   // the ONE usage constant — every path lists the same flags
   334	  const nameCheck = require('./resolve').validateChangeName(opts.change);
   335	  if (!nameCheck.ok) return err(res, `invalid change name '${opts.change}' (${nameCheck.kind}) — bare kebab-case, not date-prefixed, not a reserved name`);
   336	  const loc = resolveChange(cwd, opts.change);
   337	  if (loc.error) return err(res, loc.error);
   338	  res.stage = loc.stage;
   339	  const flowPath = path.join(loc.dir, 'flow-state.md');
   340	  // mapping m1: a hotfix bundle is not this gate's object. It is refused with a pointer at
   341	  // its own preflight rather than adapted — the seven checks below stay exactly as they are.
   342	  const hotfixPath = path.join(loc.dir, 'hotfix-state.md');
   343	  if (fs.existsSync(hotfixPath)) {
   344	    return err(res, fs.existsSync(flowPath)
   345	      ? `'${opts.change}' carries BOTH flow-state.md and hotfix-state.md — a bundle has one identity; delete the one that does not belong`
   346	      : `'${opts.change}' is a hotfix bundle, not a formal change — its mechanical exit conditions are \`apriori hotfix archive ${opts.change}\` (the gate's seven checks do not apply to the lane)`);
   347	  }
   348	  if (!fs.existsSync(flowPath)) return err(res, `no readable flow-state.md at ${flowPath} — tier-aware checks are impossible`);
   349	  let state;
   350	  try { state = parseFlowState(fs.readFileSync(flowPath, 'utf8')); }
   351	  catch (e) { return err(res, `flow-state unreadable: ${e.message}`); }
   352	  const tc = resolveTestCmd(opts, cwd);
   353	  if (tc.kind === 'error') return err(res, tc.error);
   354	
   355	  const idPattern = opts.idPattern === undefined ? null : opts.idPattern;
   356	  const b = tc.kind === 'absent'
   357	    ? checkBindingSkipped(cwd, opts.change, loc.stage, idPattern)
   358	    : checkBinding(cwd, opts.change, loc.stage, tc.value, idPattern);
   359	  if (b.infra) { for (const e of b.infra) res.errors.push(`verify: ${e}`); res.result = 'ERROR'; res.code = 2; return res; }
   360	  res.checks.push(b.check);
   361	  const tier = TIER_ENUM.includes(state.tier) ? state.tier : null;
   362	  res.checks.push(checkTasks(loc.dir, tier));
   363	  res.checks.push(checkFlowState(state, opts.change));
   364	  const rdDefect = reviewDirDefect(loc.dir);
   365	  if (rdDefect) {
   366	    res.checks.push({ id: 'C4', status: 'blocked', detail: rdDefect });
   367	    res.checks.push({ id: 'C5', status: 'blocked', detail: rdDefect });
   368	  } else {
   369	    res.checks.push(checkLedger(tier, loc.stage, loc.dir));
   370	    res.checks.push(checkEvidence(loc.dir));
   371	  }
   372	
   373	  res.checks.push(checkKb(cwd, loc.dir));
   374	  res.checks.push(checkCas(b.projection, loc.stage, !!opts.noCas, cwd));
   375	
   376	  // ERROR(2) > BLOCKED(1) > INCOMPLETE(3) > PASS(0). The ERROR rung is served by the early
   377	  // returns above, so only the lower three are decided here. `blocked` counts blocked only:
   378	  // an unrun check is not a block, and must not inflate the number a human reads.
   379	  res.blocked = res.checks.filter((c) => c.status === 'blocked').length;
   380	  const skipped = res.checks.some((c) => c.status === 'skipped');
   381	  res.result = res.blocked ? 'BLOCKED' : skipped ? 'INCOMPLETE' : 'PASS';
   382	  res.code = res.blocked ? 1 : skipped ? 3 : 0;
   383	  return res;
   384	}
   385	
   386	function toJson(res) {
   387	  return { change: res.change, stage: res.stage, checks: res.checks, result: res.result, blocked: res.blocked, errors: res.errors };
   388	}
   389	
   390	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori gate --change <name> [--test-cmd "<cmd>"] [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json] [--no-cas]';
   391	
   392	function cli(argv) {
   393	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'gate', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   394	    flags: { '--change': 'value', '--test-cmd': 'value', '--id-pattern': 'value', '--cwd': 'value', '--json': 'flag', '--no-cas': 'flag' },
   395	    jsonError: (m) => JSON.stringify({ change: null, stage: null, checks: [], result: 'ERROR', blocked: 0, errors: [m] }, null, 2) }, (f) => {
   396	    const a = { change: f['--change'] || null, testCmd: ('--test-cmd' in f) ? f['--test-cmd'] : null,   // PRESENCE, not truthiness — an empty flag is an error, never a config fallback
   397	      idPattern: ('--id-pattern' in f) ? f['--id-pattern'] : null,   // PRESENCE, not truthiness (GT-24 empty flag)
   398	      cwd: f['--cwd'] || process.cwd(), json: !!f['--json'], noCas: !!f['--no-cas'] };
   399	    const res = runGate(a);
   400	    if (a.json) { console.log(JSON.stringify(toJson(res), null, 2)); return res.code; }
   401	    for (const e of res.errors) console.error('gate: ' + e);
   402	    const mark = { pass: '✓', blocked: '✗', 'n/a': '–', skipped: '○' };
   403	    for (const c of res.checks) console.log(`${mark[c.status]} ${c.id} ${c.status === 'blocked' ? 'BLOCKED — ' : ''}${c.detail}`);
   404	    if (res.code === 0) console.log(`\nGATE: PASS — ${CAVEAT}`);
   405	    else if (res.code === 1) console.log(`\nGATE: BLOCKED (${res.blocked} item(s))`);
   406	    else if (res.code === 3) console.log('\nGATE: INCOMPLETE — C1 did not run; PASS was not reached');
   407	    return res.code;
   408	  });
   409	}
   410	
   411	module.exports = { runGate, resolveChange, classifyStatus, cli };
   500	  }
   501	  const errs = extras.errors || [];
   502	  lines.push('\nRESULT: ' + (errs.length ? 'ERROR — run not trustworthy (see stderr)'
   503	    : (v.clean && !(extras.duplicates || []).length ? 'GREEN — spec is the test suite' : 'GAPS')));
   504	  return lines.join('\n');
   505	}
   506	
   507	// Projection inputs for `verify --change` (all failure classes → run.errors, exit 2).
   508	// Roots resolve against cwd exactly as `apriori status` resolves change dirs.
   509	function buildChangeProjection(change, cwd) {
   510	  const changesDir = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes');
   511	  const storeRoot = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'specs');
   512	  const projection = { change, modules: [], conflicts: [], unstampedMutations: [] };
   513	  const d = am.discoverDeltas(changesDir, change);
   514	  if (d.errors.length) return { projection, errors: d.errors, texts: null };
   515	  const p = am.buildProjection(storeRoot, d.files, change);
   516	  projection.modules = p.modules;
   517	  projection.conflicts = p.conflicts;
   518	  projection.unstampedMutations = p.unstampedMutations;      // WARN grade — cli prints, verdict untouched
   519	  projection.notes = p.notes;
   520	  const errors = [...p.validation, ...p.hygiene, ...p.casMismatches];
   521	  if (p.conflicts.length) errors.push(`projection has ${p.conflicts.length} merge conflict(s) — no trustworthy projection: ${p.conflicts.join('; ')}`);
   522	  // deltaOps/modifiedBlocks travel with the SAME parse snapshot the projection used (never re-read)
   523	  return { projection, errors, texts: errors.length ? null : p.texts, deltaOps: p.deltaOps, modifiedBlocks: p.modifiedBlocks };
   524	}
   525	
   526	// Two test seams over the projection builder and the test-command runner. They exist so a
   527	// SECOND consumer (gate's skipped-C1 path) can be proven to share this file's one projection
   528	// implementation: a plain export could not, because verify() calls the function through its
   529	// LEXICAL binding — replacing module.exports.buildChangeProjection would not touch that call.
   530	// Every consumer therefore resolves the reference AT CALL TIME through current*(). Both
   531	// overrides default to null, so a configured run is byte-identical to its pre-seam form.
   532	let projectionBuilderOverride = null;
   533	function _setProjectionBuilder(fn) { projectionBuilderOverride = fn; }
   534	function currentProjectionBuilder() { return projectionBuilderOverride || buildChangeProjection; }
   535	let testRunnerOverride = null;
   536	function _setTestRunner(fn) { testRunnerOverride = fn; }
   537	// deliberately NOT exported — only verify() reads it; gate never runs a test command
   538	function currentTestRunner() { return testRunnerOverride || runTestCommand; }
   539	
   540	// ---- change scope (verify --change): requirement-block provenance over the projection ----
   541	// Collect every scenario occurrence of the projection ONCE, tagging each pair with whether
   542	// it lives inside a change-scoped block (produced/renamed by ADDED/MODIFIED/RENAMED; blocks
   543	// whose final projected state is deprecated are excluded — they are stripped before scanning).
   544	function collectChangePairs(texts, deltaOps) {
   545	  const pairs = [];                       // [label, title, scoped]
   546	  const requirements = [];                // [{file, name, operations}]
   547	  for (const [label, text] of texts) {
   548	    const clean = am.stripDeprecatedBlocks(stripFences(text));
   549	    const ops = deltaOps.get(label);
   550	    if (!ops) {
   551	      const sub = [];
   552	      scanTitles(clean, label, sub, true);
   553	      pairs.push(...sub.map(([l, t]) => [l, t, false]));
   554	      continue;
   555	    }
   556	    const removed = new Set(ops.removed);
   557	    const renamedNew = ops.renamedPairs.map(([, n]) => n);
   558	    const scopeNames = new Set([...ops.added, ...ops.modified, ...renamedNew].filter((n) => !removed.has(n)));
   559	    const { map } = am.parseRequirementsStrict(clean);
   560	    const firstBlock = clean.search(/^###\s+Requirement:/m);
   561	    const preamble = firstBlock >= 0 ? clean.slice(0, firstBlock) : clean;
   562	    const sub = [];
   563	    scanTitles(preamble, label, sub, true);
   564	    pairs.push(...sub.map(([l, t]) => [l, t, false]));
   565	    for (const [name, block] of map) {
   566	      const scoped = scopeNames.has(name);
   567	      if (scoped) {
   568	        const operations = [];
   569	        if (renamedNew.includes(name)) operations.push('RENAMED');
   570	        if (ops.added.includes(name)) operations.push('ADDED');
   571	        if (ops.modified.includes(name)) operations.push('MODIFIED');
   572	        requirements.push({ file: label, name, operations });
   573	      }
   574	      const bsub = [];
   575	      scanTitles(block, label, bsub, true);
   576	      pairs.push(...bsub.map(([l, t]) => [l, t, scoped]));
   577	    }
   578	  }
   579	  requirements.sort((a, b) => (a.file === b.file ? (a.name < b.name ? -1 : 1) : (a.file < b.file ? -1 : 1)));
   580	  return { pairs, requirements };
   581	}
   582	
   583	// per-kind op counts for the vacuous note: '(removal-only change)' or '(ops: ADDED 1, REMOVED 2)'
   584	function vacuousNoteFor(deltaOps) {
   585	  const counts = { ADDED: 0, MODIFIED: 0, RENAMED: 0, REMOVED: 0 };
   586	  for (const ops of deltaOps.values()) {
   587	    counts.ADDED += ops.added.length; counts.MODIFIED += ops.modified.length;
   588	    counts.RENAMED += ops.renamedPairs.length; counts.REMOVED += ops.removed.length;
   589	  }
   590	  const removalOnly = counts.REMOVED > 0 && !counts.ADDED && !counts.MODIFIED && !counts.RENAMED;
   591	  if (removalOnly) return '0 scenario(s) in change scope (removal-only change)';
   592	  const parts = Object.entries(counts).filter(([, n]) => n > 0).map(([k, n]) => `${k} ${n}`);
   593	  return `0 scenario(s) in change scope (ops: ${parts.join(', ')})`;
   594	}
   595	
   596	// an errors-only run shape shared by every pre-test early exit (failed projection,
   597	// invalid pattern, matcher-channel failure) — fail closed WITHOUT running any tests
   598	function errorsRun(errors, projection) {
   599	  const verdict = evaluate(new Map(), [], new Map(), []);
   600	  const run = { verdict, results: new Map(), fileCount: 0, failCount: 0, scenarioCount: 0,
   601	    missingTargets: [], duplicates: [], bailout: null, noTap: false, tapProblems: [], stderr: '',
   602	    exec: { status: null, signal: null, error: null }, errors };
   603	  if (projection) run.projection = projection;
   604	  return run;
   605	}
   606	
   607	// enumeration-only projection metadata for the invalid-pattern early exit on --change runs:
   608	// SR-23's projection contract survives, yet no delta CONTENT is read (directory listing only)
   609	function enumerateProjection(change, cwd) {
   610	  const changesDir = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes');
   611	  const projection = { change, modules: [], conflicts: [], unstampedMutations: [] };
   612	  const d = am.discoverDeltas(changesDir, change);
   613	  if (!d.errors.length) projection.modules = d.files.map((f) => f.suffix);
   614	  return projection;
   615	}
   616	
   617	// Full verify run. opts: { specs:[], change, testCmd, idPattern, cwd }
   618	function verify(opts) {
   619	  const cwd = opts.cwd || '.';
   620	  // pattern resolution FIRST — before any spec content is read or any test command spawned
   621	  const resolved = resolveIdPattern(cwd, opts.idPattern === undefined ? null : opts.idPattern);
   622	  if (resolved.error)
   623	    return errorsRun([resolved.error], opts.change ? enumerateProjection(opts.change, cwd) : null);
   624	  const idRe = new RegExp(resolved.source);
   625	  const matcher = makeIdMatcher(resolved);
   626	  let collected, projection = null, changeMeta = null;
   627	  if (opts.change) {
   628	    const b = currentProjectionBuilder()(opts.change, cwd);
   629	    projection = b.projection;
   630	    if (!b.texts) return errorsRun(b.errors, projection);
   631	    // one pass over the projection: every occurrence collected once, tagged with its
   632	    // change-scope provenance; ONE matcher batch serves both views (SR-63).
   633	    // Sibling active changes' delta scenario titles join the SAME batch — a failing
   634	    // orphan whose ID a sibling declares is provably that change's business (GT-26).
   635	    const { pairs, requirements } = collectChangePairs(b.texts, b.deltaOps);
   636	    const siblingTitles = collectSiblingTitles(cwd, opts.change);
   637	    // OLD-block scenario titles ride the SAME batch (integrity report input; bind nothing)
   638	    const integrityTitles = [];
   639	    for (const list of (b.modifiedBlocks || new Map()).values())
   640	      for (const { oldBlock } of list) scanTitles(oldBlock, '(integrity)', integrityTitles);
   641	    const integrityTitleTexts = integrityTitles.map(([, t]) => t);
   642	    const batch = makeThenBind(pairs, matcher, siblingTitles, integrityTitleTexts);
   643	    if (batch.failure) return errorsRun([matcherFailureMsg(batch.failure)], projection);
   644	    collected = { byId: batch.acc.byId, unidentified: batch.acc.unidentified,
   645	      files: [...b.texts.keys()], missingTargets: [], duplicates: dupsOf(batch.acc) };
   646	    const titleId = new Map();
   647	    pairs.forEach(([, t], i) => { if (!titleId.has(t)) titleId.set(t, batch.ids[i]); });
   648	    integrityTitleTexts.forEach((t, i) => { if (!titleId.has(t)) titleId.set(t, batch.integrityIds[i]); });
   649	    changeMeta = { pairs, ids: batch.ids, requirements, deltaOps: b.deltaOps, siblingIds: batch.siblingIds,
   650	      modifiedBlocks: b.modifiedBlocks, titleId };
   651	  } else {
   652	    collected = collectScenarios(opts.specs, idRe, matcher);
   653	    if (collected.failure) return errorsRun([matcherFailureMsg(collected.failure)], projection);
   654	  }
   655	  const { byId, unidentified, files, missingTargets, duplicates } = collected;
   656	  const exec = currentTestRunner()(opts.testCmd, cwd);
   657	  const parsed = parseTap(exec.out, idRe, matcher);
   658	  if (parsed.matchFailure) {
   659	    const run = errorsRun([matcherFailureMsg(parsed.matchFailure)], projection);
   660	    run.exec = { status: exec.status, signal: exec.signal, error: exec.error };
   661	    run.stderr = exec.stderr || '';
   662	    return run;
   663	  }
   664	  const { results, untagged, untaggedFails, unattributedFailures, bailout, plans, points, dupNumbers, tapProblems } = parsed;
   665	  const verdict = evaluate(byId, unidentified, results, unattributedFailures);
   666	  const failCount = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.fail, 0) + untaggedFails;
   667	  const run = {
   668	    verdict, results, fileCount: files.length, failCount,
   669	    scenarioCount: byId.size + unidentified.length,
   670	    missingTargets, duplicates, bailout, plans, points, dupNumbers,
   671	    noTap: zeroTapParsed(exec.out, results, untagged, unattributedFailures)
   672	      || (!exec.out.trim() && looksLikeTap(exec.stderr)),  // TAP went to stderr — same zero-TAP cure (2>&1)
   673	    tapProblems,
   674	    stderr: exec.stderr || '',
   675	    exec: { status: exec.status, signal: exec.signal, error: exec.error },
   676	  };
   677	  if (projection) run.projection = projection;
   678	  run.errors = infraErrors(run);
   679	  // a named scope judges that set and nothing else (SR-69..72); an untrustworthy run
   680	  // yields no scoped verdict at all rather than a satisfiable-looking one
   681	  if (opts.scope) run.scopedVerdict = run.errors.length ? null : scopedEvaluate(byId, results, opts.scope);
   682	  // change-scoped verdict split (SR-56..62): only on trustworthy --change runs; every
   683	  // ERROR class leaves the run without storeReport/changeScope (absent, never null)
   684	  if (changeMeta && run.errors.length === 0) {
   685	    applyChangeScope(run, changeMeta);
   686	    // the modified-block integrity report (informative; ALWAYS present on GREEN/GAPS)
   687	    const idOf = (title) => { const v = changeMeta.titleId.get(title); return v === undefined ? null : v; };
   688	    const entries = [];
   689	    for (const [file, list] of (changeMeta.modifiedBlocks || new Map()))
   690	      for (const { name, oldBlock, newBlock } of list)
   800	      idPattern: ('--id-pattern' in f) ? f['--id-pattern'] : null,   // PRESENCE, not truthiness (SR-52 empty flag)
   801	      cwd: f['--cwd'] || '.', json: !!f['--json'] };
   802	    if (a.change && a.specs.length) {
   803	      console.error('verify: --specs cannot be combined with --change — the projection defines the spec set');
   804	      return 2;
   805	    }
   806	    if (!a.testCmd) {
   807	      const c = configTestCmd(a.cwd);                    // init --test-cmd persists it there
   808	      if (c && c.error) { console.error(`verify: ${c.error}`); return 2; }
   809	      a.testCmd = c;
   810	    }
   811	    if ((!a.specs.length && !a.change) || !a.testCmd) { console.error(USAGE); return 2; }
   812	    const run = verify(a);
   813	    if (run.projection)
   814	      for (const sfx of run.projection.unstampedMutations || []) console.error('warning: ' + am.unstampedWarning(sfx));
   815	    if (run.errors.length && !a.json)
   816	      for (const e of run.errors) console.error('error: ' + e);
   817	    console.log(a.json ? JSON.stringify(verifyJson(run), null, 2)
   818	                       : formatReport(run.verdict, run.results, run.fileCount, { duplicates: run.duplicates, errors: run.errors, stderr: run.stderr, storeReport: run.storeReport, vacuousNote: run.vacuousNote, modifiedIntegrity: run.modifiedIntegrity }));
   819	    if (run.errors.length) return 2;
   820	    return run.verdict.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0 ? 0 : 1;
   821	  });

   824	module.exports = { leadId, stripFences, collectScenarios, collectScenariosFromTexts, parseTap, looksLikeTap, scopedEvaluate,
   825	  zeroTapParsed, runTestCommand, evaluate, infraErrors, formatReport, verifyJson, verify,
   826	  configTestCmd, cli, DEFAULT_ID, makeIdMatcher, _setChildRunner, matcherFailureMsg, CHILD_SPAWN_OPTS,
   827	  buildChangeProjection, currentProjectionBuilder, _setProjectionBuilder, _setTestRunner };
   120	    }
   121	    if (!broken) checks.push({ id: 'D4', status: 'ok', detail: `pointers intact for: ${detected.join(', ')}` });
   122	  }
   123	
   124	  // D6 is COMPUTED before the D5 probe (the id-pattern must be validated — and its store
   125	  // scan survived — before any test command runs; display order stays D5 then D6): an
   126	  // uncompilable or terminated config pattern skips the probe entirely.
   127	  const specsDir = path.join(ap, 'specs');
   128	  const d6Entries = [];
   129	  let idPatternBroken = false;
   130	  const idp = resolveIdPattern(cwd, null);
   131	  if (idp.error) {
   132	    idPatternBroken = true;
   133	    d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'finding', detail: idp.error, fix: 'fix the id-pattern row in apriori/process-config.md' });
   134	  } else if (!fs.existsSync(specsDir)) d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no store dir — see D2' });
   135	  else {
   136	    const collected = collectScenarios([specsDir], new RegExp(idp.source), makeIdMatcher(idp));
   137	    if (collected.failure) {
   138	      idPatternBroken = true;
   139	      d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'finding', detail: matcherFailureMsg(collected.failure), fix: 'fix the id-pattern row in apriori/process-config.md' });
   140	    } else {
   141	      const { byId, unidentified, files, duplicates } = collected;
   142	      const src = ` (id-pattern: ${idp.origin})`;
   143	      if (!files.length || (byId.size === 0 && unidentified.length === 0))
   144	        d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'n/a', detail: 'empty store — normal for a new project' });
   145	      else {
   146	        let bad = 0;
   147	        if (unidentified.length) { d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'finding', detail: `scenario(s) without a bindable ${boundedSource(idp.source)} ID${src}: ${unidentified.map(([, t]) => t.slice(0, 40)).join(' · ')}`, fix: 'add leading IDs' }); bad++; }
   148	        if (duplicates.length) { d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'finding', detail: `duplicate scenario IDs: ${duplicates.map((d) => d.id).join(', ')}`, fix: 'deduplicate IDs' }); bad++; }
   149	        if (!bad) d6Entries.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'ok', detail: `${byId.size} scenario(s), all bindable, no duplicates${src}` });
   150	      }
   151	    }
   152	  }
   153	
   154	  // D5 — TAP probe (the one declared side effect; --no-run and a broken id-pattern skip)
   155	  const cfgCmd = opts.testCmd ? null : configTestCmd(cwd);
   156	  const testCmd = opts.testCmd || (cfgCmd && cfgCmd.error ? null : cfgCmd);
   157	  if (idPatternBroken) checks.push({ id: 'D5', status: 'n/a', detail: 'probe skipped (invalid id-pattern config)' });
   158	  else if (cfgCmd && cfgCmd.error) checks.push({ id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `config: ${cfgCmd.error}`, fix: 'keep one live test-cmd row' });
   159	  // --no-run is judged BEFORE the missing command: an explicit human skip is never a finding,
   160	  // and the missing-command branch below is one.
   161	  else if (opts.noRun) checks.push({ id: 'D5', status: 'n/a', detail: 'probe skipped (--no-run)' });
   162	  // A missing test command is not a neutral absence: it takes `apriori gate` with it. Reporting
   163	  // n/a here is what let a project run a whole change with a gate that never once ran.
   164	  else if (!testCmd) checks.push({ id: 'D5', status: 'finding',
   165	    detail: 'no test command configured — `apriori gate` cannot run C1 (the binding check) at all',
   166	    fix: 'add a test-cmd row to apriori/process-config.md (or pass --test-cmd)' });
   167	  else checks.push(classifyProbe(runTestCommand(testCmd, cwd)));
   168	
   169	  // D6 — store health (computed above, displayed here)
   170	  checks.push(...d6Entries);
   171	
   172	  // D7 — changes overview
   173	  const actives = activeChanges(cwd);
   174	  const notes = [];
   175	  let d7bad = 0;
   176	  const changesRoot = path.join(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes');
   177	  for (const c of actives) {
   178	    const fp = path.join(changesRoot, c, 'flow-state.md');
   179	    if (!fs.existsSync(fp)) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: no flow-state.md`, fix: 'apriori new / restore the file' }); d7bad++; continue; }
   180	    // per-file containment: a symlinked flow-state pointing outside the changes tree is
   181	    // diagnosed, never read (doctor must not leak outside content into its report)
   182	    if (!containsReal(changesRoot, fp)) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: flow-state.md escapes apriori/changes/ (symlink?) — not read`, fix: 'fix the file' }); d7bad++; continue; }
   183	    let st;
   184	    try { st = parseFlowState(fs.readFileSync(fp, 'utf8')); }
   185	    catch (e) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: flow-state unreadable (${e.message})`, fix: 'fix the file' }); d7bad++; continue; }
   186	    if (!st.change) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: flow-state has no 'change:' key`, fix: 'fix the file' }); d7bad++; }
   187	    else if (st.change !== c) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: flow-state says 'change: ${st.change}' — mismatches the dir`, fix: 'fix the file' }); d7bad++; }
   188	    else notes.push(`${c} @ ${st['current-step'] || '?'}`);
   189	  }
   190	  const archRoot = path.join(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes', 'archive');
   191	  if (fs.existsSync(archRoot)) {
   192	    for (const b of fs.readdirSync(archRoot).sort()) {
   193	      if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T\d{4}-/.test(b)) continue;
   194	      const dir = path.join(archRoot, b);
   195	      let isDir = false;
   196	      try { isDir = fs.statSync(dir).isDirectory(); } catch { continue; }
   197	      if (!isDir) continue;
   198	      if (!containsReal(archRoot, dir)) { notes.push(`${b}: skipped (escapes archive/ — symlink?)`); continue; }
   199	      const fp = path.join(dir, 'flow-state.md');
   200	      if (!fs.existsSync(fp)) continue;
   201	      if (!containsReal(archRoot, fp)) { notes.push(`${b}: flow-state escapes archive/ (symlink?) — not read`); continue; }
   202	      const st = parseFlowState(fs.readFileSync(fp, 'utf8'));
   203	      const step = st['current-step'];
   204	      if (step && step !== 'DONE' && step !== 'ABANDONED') notes.push(`archived ${b} @ ${step} — gate ④ possibly pending`);
   205	    }
   206	  }
   207	  if (!d7bad) checks.push(actives.length || notes.length
   208	    ? { id: 'D7', status: 'ok', detail: notes.join('; ') || `${actives.length} active change(s), flow-states valid` }
   209	    : { id: 'D7', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no changes yet' });
   210	  else if (notes.length) checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'ok', detail: notes.join('; ') });
   211	
   212	  // D8 — legacy 3.x layout roots (existence-level, never followed or read through)
   213	  const legacy = legacyRoots(cwd);
   214	  checks.push(legacy.length
   215	    ? { id: 'D8', status: 'finding', detail: `legacy 3.x layout root(s) present: ${legacy.join(', ')}`, fix: 'migrate the artifacts into their change bundles — see MIGRATING.md (4.0): https://github.com/Apriorhythm/apriori-spec-development/blob/main/MIGRATING.md' }
   216	    : { id: 'D8', status: 'ok', detail: 'no legacy 3.x layout roots' });
   217	
   218	  return finish();
   219	}

   223	}

test('SR-75 the seams are inert by default', () => {
  const root = noCmdProject();
  assert.strictEqual(sr.currentProjectionBuilder(), sr.buildChangeProjection, 'default resolves to the real builder');
  const marker = () => ({ projection: {}, errors: ['x'], texts: null });
  try {
    sr._setProjectionBuilder(marker);
    assert.strictEqual(sr.currentProjectionBuilder(), marker);
  } finally { sr._setProjectionBuilder(null); }
  assert.strictEqual(sr.currentProjectionBuilder(), sr.buildChangeProjection, 'clearing restores the default exactly');
  assert.strictEqual(gate.runGate({ cwd: root, change: 'c' }).code, 3, 'behaviour is unchanged after a clear');
  assert.ok(!('currentTestRunner' in sr), 'the test-runner resolver stays module-private');
});

docs/cli.md:99:`--change` runs are **change-scoped**: the verdict (exit 0/1) judges only this change's requirement blocks (their scenarios bound green, no scoped duplicate/unidentified, no unprovable failure signal — an ID-less failure or a failing ID that no sibling active change declares still blocks, fail-closed); a red bound to an out-of-scope scenario, or one attributed to a sibling change's cleanly-parsed delta (only its ADDED/MODIFIED block scenarios grant the exemption), never blocks. The same run prints an informative **store report** (whole projection, six classes) so parallel changes go green independently while historical gaps stay visible. `--change --json` adds `storeReport`, `changeScope` and `modifiedIntegrity` on GREEN/GAPS (absent on every ERROR); `--specs` output is byte-identical to before. `modifiedIntegrity` reports every MODIFIED block's replacement fidelity (retained/titleChanged/dropped/added/ambiguous scenarios plus lost lines, requirement prose included) — informative only, never a verdict change; the human `— MODIFIED INTEGRITY —` section prints when a risk class is non-empty.
docs/cli.md:180:`| verification-profile | ui / backend / fullstack / docs / none |` declares once, for the whole project, what kind of thing this repository is — and therefore what evidence its verification asks for. The row is **human-owned**: agents read it, never write it, exactly like `test-cmd`.
docs/concepts.md:21:> **Without the system knowledge base, the Agent can only reverse-engineer abstract intent from the code — slow, and easy to guess wrong.** This is exactly the core tension Section 6 ("Legacy Project Development") is meant to resolve.
docs/concepts.md:65:**An honest caveat on LLM judges.** Heterogeneity reduces bias but does not eliminate it: self-preference in LLM judges is driven by *familiarity* (perplexity), not authorship — a different model only partially escapes it; code defects are partly **shared, systemic weaknesses across models**, so a cross-model reviewer inherits some of the producer's blind spots; and in one four-tool review comparison, 93.4% of distinct findings were caught by exactly one tool — review coverage is inherently incomplete. The implication: deterministic verification stays the primary instrument, always.
docs/concepts.md:182:> ⚠️ Don't point a weaker model at a stronger one's hard reasoning — auditing an Opus design with Haiku tends to miss exactly the subtle issues Haiku can't follow. Review *sideways or down* in capability, not steeply up. (`/model` switches the current session, but for review always **start a new session** so the reviewer keeps fresh eyes.)
docs/concepts.md:426:Every loop above already has a **machine-checkable exit condition** — which is exactly what Claude Code's `/goal` consumes. `/goal "<condition>"` makes Claude work across turns **unattended until the condition holds**; after each turn an independent fast model (Haiku) reads the transcript and decides done / not-done, looping until done or you stop it.
docs/concepts.md:597:One cumulative ledger per change, `apriori/changes/<change>/review/issues.md` (format: RUNBOOK **P0**). Reviews follow a **scope discipline** (per Anthropic's fully-verified warning that gap-hunting reviewers report gaps even in sound work): only correctness/security/stated-requirement gaps become formal rows; the rest are `advisory` — per-item lists stay in the review doc, the ledger takes one batch row per round. Why it exists: cross-round memory lives in a file instead of a session, so every round's reviewer can be a **fresh** session without losing the thread ([§1.4](#14-adversarial-review)). Who writes what: the reviewer appends rows and flips `fixed → verified`; the producer flips `open → fixed/rejected` — a rejection must carry a reason, because human gates read the rejections first. A re-found issue **reopens its old ID** rather than getting a new row; that reopened ID is exactly the oscillation alarm [§4.10](#410-automating-the-loop-with-goal-claude-code) watches for.

Two test-only injection seams SHALL exist: one over the projection builder and one over the test-command runner. Both default to the real functions, leaving every configured path byte-identical.

apriori/changes/gate-degrades/specs/gate/spec.md:6:`apriori gate --change <name>` SHALL evaluate the machine-checkable gate conditions for exactly one change and encode the aggregate in its exit code: 0 = every applicable check passed (`GATE: PASS`), 1 = at least one check blocked (`GATE: BLOCKED (<n> item(s))`), 2 = the evaluation itself is untrustworthy (usage error, invalid or escaping name, change found nowhere, unreadable flow-state, an unusable test-command source, or an untrustworthy C1 verify run), 3 = every check that COULD run passed but at least one was skipped (`GATE: INCOMPLETE`). The aggregate is a strict total order: ERROR(2) outranks BLOCKED(1) outranks INCOMPLETE(3) outranks PASS(0) — a confirmed block is never softened by an unrun check, and `blocked` counts `blocked` statuses only, never `skipped`. It SHALL be strictly read-only and SHALL state in its output that PASS covers mechanical checks only — human gates remain human. apriori/changes/gate-degrades/specs/gate/spec.md:83:- THEN the result is `BLOCKED` with exit code 1 and `blocked` counts only the blocked checks — the skipped C1 never softens a confirmed block, and never inflates the count

apriori/changes/gate-degrades/specs/gate/spec.md:111:- THEN C1 is `skipped`, C7 is `–` (deltas already merged), C4 still demands every ledger row be terminal, and the exit code follows the same total order; the shared projection builder is invoked ZERO times on this path, observably — an archived bundle's deltas are already in the store, so building a projection could only manufacture a false block
apriori/changes/gate-degrades/specs/doctor/spec.md:30:- THEN D5 classifies: spawn error, signal kill, `Bail out!`, empty output, zero parsed TAP without a version/plan line (naming `--test-reporter=tap`), a TAP version or non-`1..0` plan with zero result lines ("truncated or malformed"), and a non-zero exit unexplained by parsed TAP failures — each a finding; parsed TAP whose failures explain any non-zero exit is ok (counts as info — test failures are NOT doctor findings), and an exactly-`1..0` plan with exit status 0 is ok ("empty suite") — a `1..0` plan with a NON-ZERO exit is the unexplained-exit finding, classified first
