 succeeded in 111ms:
   130	if (errors.length)                    → ERROR,      exit 2
   131	else if (blocked > 0)                 → BLOCKED,    exit 1        // blocked = status==='blocked' 的条数
   132	else if (any check status==='skipped')→ INCOMPLETE, exit 3
   133	else                                  → PASS,       exit 0
   134	```
   135	`blocked` 字段的语义不变：**只数 `blocked`，不数 `skipped`**。
   136	
   137	### B3 — doctor：把「未配置」的后果说出来
   138	
   139	完全未配置测试命令时，D5 由 `n/a` 升为 `finding`，其 detail 必须点明**后果**
   140	（`apriori gate` 的 C1 无法执行），`fix` 指向 `process-config.md` 的 `test-cmd` 行。
   141	
   142	人类显式要求跳过（`--no-run`）时**仍是 `n/a`**——显式跳过不是缺陷。
   143	
   144	### B4 — 有配置时零变化
   145	
   146	配置齐备的项目，本 change 后 gate 与 doctor 的检查集合、每项 detail 文案、
   147	`--json` 的**键集与取值**、退出码**完全不变**。
   148	
   149	---
   150	
   151	## 三、判定矩阵（r1·REQ-2 的处置，r2 收窄）
   152	
   153	### 3.1 测试命令来源的分类（互斥且穷举）
   154	
   155	判定按 T1→T7 顺序，**首个匹配者胜**。
   156	
   157	| # | 输入 | 分类 | 结果 |
   158	|---|---|---|---|
   159	| T1 | `--test-cmd` **存在**，且值含**至少一个非空白字符** | 有效（flag） | 照常执行，与本 change 前一致 |
   160	| T2 | `--test-cmd` **存在**，值为空字符串 `""` | **flag 来源错误** | ERROR / exit 2；**绝不**回落到 config、绝不降级为 skipped（与 D-GT-5 对 `--id-pattern` 的存在性判定同构） |
   161	| T3 | `--test-cmd` **存在**，值仅由空白字符组成 | **flag 来源错误** | 同 T2——一个只有空白的命令无法执行；若静默降级为「缺席」，一个手滑的引号就会把 ERROR 变成 INCOMPLETE |
   162	| T4 | 无 flag，`process-config.md` **存在但不可读**（目录、权限、不可达祖先——非 ENOENT 的任何失败） | **配置错误** | ERROR / exit 2（现状不变，由 `readConfig` 的既有分类产生） |
   163	| T5 | 无 flag，config 的 `test-cmd` 行**冲突**（同键不同值） | **配置错误** | ERROR / exit 2（现状不变）——「配置坏了」与「配置没有」是两件事 |
   164	| T6 | 无 flag，config 有合法 `test-cmd` 行（值含至少一个非空白字符） | 有效（config） | 照常执行，与本 change 前一致 |
   165	| T7 | 其余一切：无 config 文件、无该行、**或该行的值为空/仅空白** | **缺席** | C1 = `skipped`，进入 §3.2 |
   166	
   167	> **T7 为何吞掉「值为空/仅空白的 config 行」（r2 勘误）**：`lib/config.js:67` 是
   168	> `if (!key || value === undefined || value === '') continue;`，且单元格在此之前已 `trim()`。
   169	> 也就是说**共享 config 解析器按契约把空值/纯空白值等同于「该行不存在」**，对所有 key 一视同仁。
   170	> v2 曾要求把它判为 ERROR——那需要改 `lib/config.js` 及其全局契约（影响每一个 key），
   171	> 既超出本 change 声明的触及范围，也会把一个 gate 的局部问题变成全仓配置语义变更。
   172	> 因此按状态 A 归入 T7。
   173	>
   174	> **flag 层严、config 层宽的不对称是刻意的**：flag 是操作者在**本次调用**中的显式动作，
   175	> 其「存在性」可观察，写错了必须立刻报错；config 行的空值则被一个管辖全部 key 的共享解析器
   176	> 提前归一化掉了，gate 在消费点已经看不见它。承认这个不对称，好过假装能区分。
   177	
   178	### 3.1b `runGate()` 层面的契约（r2·REQ-2 新增）
   179	
   180	flag 的**存在性**只有 CLI 层知道，因此 `runGate` 的 `testCmd` 取值域必须显式定义：
   181	
   182	| `opts.testCmd` | 含义 | 归入 |
   183	|---|---|---|
   184	| 字段缺席 / `undefined` / `null` | flag 未出现 | 走 T4..T7 的 config 解析 |
   185	| `''` 或仅空白字符串 | flag 出现但值非法 | T2 / T3 → ERROR / exit 2 |
   186	| 含非空白字符的字符串 | flag 有效 | T1 |
   187	
   188	| 任何其它类型（数字 / 数组 / 对象 / 布尔） | 契约外输入 | **ERROR / exit 2**，消息点明类型；绝不交给 `spawnSync` 去抛类型错误 |
   189	
   190	CLI 层据此改为**存在性**传递：`testCmd: ('--test-cmd' in f) ? f['--test-cmd'] : null`
   191	（现状是 `f['--test-cmd'] || null`，它把 `''` 塌缩成 `null`，正是 T2 无法被区分的原因）。
   192	这与 `--id-pattern` 既有的存在性写法（`lib/spec-runner.js:786`，SR-52 / D-GT-5）完全同构。
   193	最后一行（非字符串）是 r3 补上的：`runGate` 是导出的公共函数，JS 里这些取值可达，
   194	留白等于把类型错误推迟到 `spawnSync` 抛异常——那是一个**没有 detail 的** exit 2。
   195	
   196	### 3.1c 本 change 需要的两个测试注入 seam（r3·REQ-1 新增）
   197	
   198	AC-GD-14 / 15b 的可机械断言性依赖两个**互不相同**的 seam，二者都必须是
   199	`verify()` 与 gate 的 T7 路径**共用的同一个可替换引用**：
   200	
   201	| seam | 包裹对象 | 用途 |
   202	|---|---|---|
   203	| `_setProjectionBuilder(fn)` | `buildChangeProjection` 的调用点 | 证明两条路径经过**同一个** builder（身份 + 次数），并让测试捕获 T7 路径内部拿到的完整 projection 对象——`runGate()` 本身不返回它 |
   204	| `_setTestRunner(fn)` | `runTestCommand` 的调用点 | 断言 T7 路径下测试进程被起 **0** 次（现状无任何注入点，`spawnSync` 直接起进程） |
   205	
   206	命名与既有 `_setChildRunner` 同族；默认值分别是现有的 `buildChangeProjection` / `runTestCommand`，
   207	**配置齐备路径上的行为一字不变**（B4）。
   208	
   209	### 3.2 「缺席」情形下的逐检查行为矩阵
   210	
   211	前置说明——`runGate` 的判定顺序（本 change 只在标注处插入，其余次序不动）：
   212	
   213	```
   214	① --change 缺失 / 名字非法 / 解析失败            → ERROR 2
   215	② hotfix bundle（mapping m1）                    → ERROR 2   ← 发生在测试命令解析之前，不受本 change 影响
   216	③ flow-state 缺失 / 不可读                       → ERROR 2
   217	④ 测试命令来源判定（§3.1）                        ← 本 change 在此改：T7 不再 ERROR，标记 c1-skipped
   218	⑤ id-pattern 解析（flag > config > 默认）         ← 本 change 在此显式化：**无论 C1 是否跑，都要解析并校验**
   219	⑥ 绑定阶段：有命令 → verify()；缺席 → 只建 projection
   220	⑦ C2..C6
   221	⑧ C7（消费 ⑥ 的 projection）
   222	```
   223	
   224	**行判定顺序（r2 新增，r3 更正）**：矩阵按 **M7→M5→M3→M4→M8→M6→M2→M1** 的**因果先后**
   225	判定，**首个匹配者胜**；下表的「情形」列因此都应读作「在此前各行都不匹配的前提下」。
   226	
   227	> **M7 必须先于 M5（r3 勘误）**：`lib/gate.js:301` 的 hotfix 识别在 `lib/gate.js:306` 的
   228	> flow-state 存在性检查**之前**。而一个正常的 hotfix bundle **本来就没有** `flow-state.md`，
   229	> 因此它同时满足 M7 与 M5；v3 写成 M5→M7 会让它落进「flow-state 缺失」的错误文案，
   230	> 违反 AC-GD-12 要求的 mapping m1 指引。
   231	
   232	| # | 情形（均以 §3.1 的 T7「缺席」为前提） | id-pattern 是否解析 | 是否建 projection | 是否启动测试进程 | C1 | C7 | C2..C6 | errors | result / exit |
   233	|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
   234	| M7 | 目标是 hotfix bundle | — | — | 否 | — | — | — | 非空 | **ERROR / 2**（② 早于 ④，且早于 ③） |
   235	| M5 | flow-state 缺失/不可读 | — | — | 否 | — | — | — | 非空 | **ERROR / 2**（发生在 ④ 之前） |
   250	  if (checked > 0) return { id: 'C6', status: 'pass', detail: `${checked} module stamp(s) up to date${notes.length ? `; ${notes.join('; ')}` : ''}` };
   251	  return { id: 'C6', status: 'n/a', detail: notes.join('; ') };
   252	}
   253	
   254	function checkBinding(cwd, name, stage, testCmd, idPattern) {
   255	  const run = stage === 'in-flight'
   256	    ? verify({ change: name, cwd, testCmd, idPattern })
   257	    : verify({ specs: [path.join(cwd, 'apriori', 'specs')], cwd, testCmd, idPattern });
   258	  if (run.errors.length) return { infra: run.errors };
   259	  const v = run.verdict;
   260	  const clean = v.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0;
   261	  // in-flight runs carry the informative store summary (change-scoped verdict, GT-26/27)
   262	  const sr2 = run.storeReport;
   263	  const suffix = sr2
   264	    ? `; store: ${sr2.boundRed.length} red, ${sr2.unbound.length} unbound, ${sr2.orphan.length} orphan, ${sr2.unidentified.length} unidentified, ${sr2.unattributedFailures.count} unattributed, ${sr2.duplicates.length} duplicate(s) outstanding`
   265	    : '';
   266	  const label0 = sr2 ? `${stage}, change-scoped` : stage;
   267	  if (clean) return { check: { id: 'C1', status: 'pass', detail: `verify GREEN (${label0})${suffix}` }, projection: run.projection };
   268	  const parts = [];
   269	  for (const [label, arr] of [['red', v.boundRed], ['unbound', v.unbound], ['orphan', v.orphan], ['unidentified', v.unidentified], ['unattributed-failures', v.unattributed || []], ['duplicate-IDs', run.duplicates]])
   270	    if (arr.length) parts.push(`${arr.length} ${label}`);
   271	  return { check: { id: 'C1', status: 'blocked', detail: `verify GAPS: ${parts.join(', ')}${suffix}` }, projection: run.projection };
   272	}
   273	
   274	
   275	// The same shape as checkBinding, for the path where no test command exists. C1 cannot run,
   276	// but everything C1 was ALSO producing for its neighbours still can: C7 needs the projection,
   277	// and a broken id-pattern is still a broken config. Nothing here spawns a process.
   278	function checkBindingSkipped(cwd, name, stage, idPattern) {
   279	  // a broken pattern is a broken CONFIG, not an absent one — it stays an evaluation error
   280	  // (GT-24 keeps holding). Compile-checking is in-process; no scenario is ever MATCHED here,
   281	  // so no matcher child is spawned and GT-25's runtime precondition cannot arise.
   282	  const idp = resolveIdPattern(cwd, idPattern);
   283	  if (idp.error) return { infra: [idp.error] };
   284	  const detail = 'skipped — no test command (pass --test-cmd or add a test-cmd row to '
   285	    + 'apriori/process-config.md); the binding check did not run';
   286	  const c1 = { id: 'C1', status: 'skipped', detail };
   287	  // archived: the deltas are already merged and C7 is n/a — building a projection could only
   288	  // manufacture a false block, so it is never built at all.
   289	  if (stage === 'archived') return { check: c1, projection: null };
   290	  const b = sr.currentProjectionBuilder()(name, cwd);
   291	  if (b.errors && b.errors.length) return { infra: b.errors };
   292	  // fail closed: no trustworthy texts is untrustworthy even when nothing said why. Reporting
   293	  // it with an empty errors list would be an ERROR that cannot explain itself.
   294	  if (!b.texts) return { infra: ['projection produced no trustworthy texts and reported no error — refusing to judge'] };
   295	  return { check: c1, projection: b.projection };
   296	}
   297	
   298	// The test command has three fates, and "broken" is not "absent". A flag that is present but
   299	// empty (or all whitespace) is an operator error in THIS invocation and must never fall back
   300	// to the config; an empty config VALUE, by contrast, was already normalised to "no such row"
   301	// by the shared reader (config.js), so gate sees an absence and treats it as one.
   302	function resolveTestCmd(opts, cwd) {
   303	  const flag = opts.testCmd;
   304	  if (flag !== undefined && flag !== null) {
   305	    if (typeof flag !== 'string') return { kind: 'error', error: `--test-cmd must be a string (got ${Array.isArray(flag) ? 'array' : typeof flag})` };
   306	    if (flag.trim() === '') return { kind: 'error', error: 'empty --test-cmd — pass a command or omit the flag' };
   307	    return { kind: 'ok', value: flag };
   308	  }
   309	  const c = configTestCmd(cwd);
   310	  if (c && c.error) return { kind: 'error', error: `verify: ${c.error}` };
   311	  if (c) return { kind: 'ok', value: c };
   312	  return { kind: 'absent' };
   313	}
   314	
   315	// C7: unstamped mutation deltas are denied by default — a waiver is always visible (GT-16).
   316	// Archived stage: the deltas are already merged; there is nothing left to stamp-check.
   317	function checkCas(projection, stage, noCas, cwd) {
   318	  if (stage === 'archived') return { id: 'C7', status: 'n/a', detail: 'deltas already merged' };
   319	  const um = (projection && projection.unstampedMutations) || [];
   320	  if (!um.length) return { id: 'C7', status: 'pass', detail: 'every mutation delta is stamped' };  // nothing to consult — silent pass (GT-16)
   321	  if (noCas) return { id: 'C7', status: 'pass', detail: 'waived (--no-cas)' };
   322	  const problem = configCasProblem(cwd);                       // consumption-time only
   323	  if (problem) return { id: 'C7', status: 'blocked', detail: `${problem} — a broken config never equals a waiver` };
   324	  if (configCas(cwd) === 'optional') return { id: 'C7', status: 'pass', detail: 'waived (process-config cas: optional)' };
   325	  return { id: 'C7', status: 'blocked',
   326	    detail: `unstamped mutation delta(s): ${um.join(', ')} — run: apriori stamp <store-file> (or waive with --no-cas / a process-config cas: optional row)` };
   327	}
   328	
   329	// The whole evaluation. → { code, stage, checks, result, blocked, errors }
   330	function runGate(opts) {
   331	  const cwd = opts.cwd || process.cwd();
   332	  const res = { code: 0, stage: null, checks: [], result: 'PASS', blocked: 0, errors: [], change: opts.change || null };
   333	  if (!opts.change) return err(res, USAGE);   // the ONE usage constant — every path lists the same flags
   334	  const nameCheck = require('./resolve').validateChangeName(opts.change);
   335	  if (!nameCheck.ok) return err(res, `invalid change name '${opts.change}' (${nameCheck.kind}) — bare kebab-case, not date-prefixed, not a reserved name`);
   336	  const loc = resolveChange(cwd, opts.change);
   337	  if (loc.error) return err(res, loc.error);
   338	  res.stage = loc.stage;
   339	  const flowPath = path.join(loc.dir, 'flow-state.md');
   340	  // mapping m1: a hotfix bundle is not this gate's object. It is refused with a pointer at
   341	  // its own preflight rather than adapted — the seven checks below stay exactly as they are.
   342	  const hotfixPath = path.join(loc.dir, 'hotfix-state.md');
   343	  if (fs.existsSync(hotfixPath)) {
   344	    return err(res, fs.existsSync(flowPath)
   345	      ? `'${opts.change}' carries BOTH flow-state.md and hotfix-state.md — a bundle has one identity; delete the one that does not belong`
   346	      : `'${opts.change}' is a hotfix bundle, not a formal change — its mechanical exit conditions are \`apriori hotfix archive ${opts.change}\` (the gate's seven checks do not apply to the lane)`);
   347	  }
   348	  if (!fs.existsSync(flowPath)) return err(res, `no readable flow-state.md at ${flowPath} — tier-aware checks are impossible`);
   349	  let state;
   350	  try { state = parseFlowState(fs.readFileSync(flowPath, 'utf8')); }
   351	  catch (e) { return err(res, `flow-state unreadable: ${e.message}`); }
   352	  const tc = resolveTestCmd(opts, cwd);
   353	  if (tc.kind === 'error') return err(res, tc.error);
   354	
   355	  const idPattern = opts.idPattern === undefined ? null : opts.idPattern;
   356	  const b = tc.kind === 'absent'
   357	    ? checkBindingSkipped(cwd, opts.change, loc.stage, idPattern)
   358	    : checkBinding(cwd, opts.change, loc.stage, tc.value, idPattern);
   359	  if (b.infra) { for (const e of b.infra) res.errors.push(`verify: ${e}`); res.result = 'ERROR'; res.code = 2; return res; }
   360	  res.checks.push(b.check);
   361	  const tier = TIER_ENUM.includes(state.tier) ? state.tier : null;
   362	  res.checks.push(checkTasks(loc.dir, tier));
   363	  res.checks.push(checkFlowState(state, opts.change));
   364	  const rdDefect = reviewDirDefect(loc.dir);
   365	  if (rdDefect) {
   366	    res.checks.push({ id: 'C4', status: 'blocked', detail: rdDefect });
   367	    res.checks.push({ id: 'C5', status: 'blocked', detail: rdDefect });
   368	  } else {
   369	    res.checks.push(checkLedger(tier, loc.stage, loc.dir));
   370	    res.checks.push(checkEvidence(loc.dir));
   371	  }
   372	
   373	  res.checks.push(checkKb(cwd, loc.dir));
   374	  res.checks.push(checkCas(b.projection, loc.stage, !!opts.noCas, cwd));
   375	
   376	  // ERROR(2) > BLOCKED(1) > INCOMPLETE(3) > PASS(0). The ERROR rung is served by the early
   377	  // returns above, so only the lower three are decided here. `blocked` counts blocked only:
   378	  // an unrun check is not a block, and must not inflate the number a human reads.
   379	  res.blocked = res.checks.filter((c) => c.status === 'blocked').length;
   380	  const skipped = res.checks.some((c) => c.status === 'skipped');
   381	  res.result = res.blocked ? 'BLOCKED' : skipped ? 'INCOMPLETE' : 'PASS';
   382	  res.code = res.blocked ? 1 : skipped ? 3 : 0;
   383	  return res;
   384	}
   385	
   386	function toJson(res) {
   387	  return { change: res.change, stage: res.stage, checks: res.checks, result: res.result, blocked: res.blocked, errors: res.errors };
   388	}
   389	
   390	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori gate --change <name> [--test-cmd "<cmd>"] [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json] [--no-cas]';
   391	
   392	function cli(argv) {
   393	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'gate', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   394	    flags: { '--change': 'value', '--test-cmd': 'value', '--id-pattern': 'value', '--cwd': 'value', '--json': 'flag', '--no-cas': 'flag' },
   395	    jsonError: (m) => JSON.stringify({ change: null, stage: null, checks: [], result: 'ERROR', blocked: 0, errors: [m] }, null, 2) }, (f) => {
   396	    const a = { change: f['--change'] || null, testCmd: ('--test-cmd' in f) ? f['--test-cmd'] : null,   // PRESENCE, not truthiness — an empty flag is an error, never a config fallback
   397	      idPattern: ('--id-pattern' in f) ? f['--id-pattern'] : null,   // PRESENCE, not truthiness (GT-24 empty flag)
   398	      cwd: f['--cwd'] || process.cwd(), json: !!f['--json'], noCas: !!f['--no-cas'] };
   399	    const res = runGate(a);
   400	    if (a.json) { console.log(JSON.stringify(toJson(res), null, 2)); return res.code; }
   401	    for (const e of res.errors) console.error('gate: ' + e);
   402	    const mark = { pass: '✓', blocked: '✗', 'n/a': '–', skipped: '○' };
   403	    for (const c of res.checks) console.log(`${mark[c.status]} ${c.id} ${c.status === 'blocked' ? 'BLOCKED — ' : ''}${c.detail}`);
   404	    if (res.code === 0) console.log(`\nGATE: PASS — ${CAVEAT}`);
   405	    else if (res.code === 1) console.log(`\nGATE: BLOCKED (${res.blocked} item(s))`);
   500	  }
   501	  const errs = extras.errors || [];
   502	  lines.push('\nRESULT: ' + (errs.length ? 'ERROR — run not trustworthy (see stderr)'
   503	    : (v.clean && !(extras.duplicates || []).length ? 'GREEN — spec is the test suite' : 'GAPS')));
   504	  return lines.join('\n');
   505	}
   506	
   507	// Projection inputs for `verify --change` (all failure classes → run.errors, exit 2).
   508	// Roots resolve against cwd exactly as `apriori status` resolves change dirs.
   509	function buildChangeProjection(change, cwd) {
   510	  const changesDir = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes');
   511	  const storeRoot = path.resolve(cwd, 'apriori', 'specs');
   512	  const projection = { change, modules: [], conflicts: [], unstampedMutations: [] };
   513	  const d = am.discoverDeltas(changesDir, change);
   514	  if (d.errors.length) return { projection, errors: d.errors, texts: null };
   515	  const p = am.buildProjection(storeRoot, d.files, change);
   516	  projection.modules = p.modules;
   517	  projection.conflicts = p.conflicts;
   518	  projection.unstampedMutations = p.unstampedMutations;      // WARN grade — cli prints, verdict untouched
   519	  projection.notes = p.notes;
   520	  const errors = [...p.validation, ...p.hygiene, ...p.casMismatches];
   521	  if (p.conflicts.length) errors.push(`projection has ${p.conflicts.length} merge conflict(s) — no trustworthy projection: ${p.conflicts.join('; ')}`);
   522	  // deltaOps/modifiedBlocks travel with the SAME parse snapshot the projection used (never re-read)
   523	  return { projection, errors, texts: errors.length ? null : p.texts, deltaOps: p.deltaOps, modifiedBlocks: p.modifiedBlocks };
   524	}
   525	
   526	// Two test seams over the projection builder and the test-command runner. They exist so a
   527	// SECOND consumer (gate's skipped-C1 path) can be proven to share this file's one projection
   528	// implementation: a plain export could not, because verify() calls the function through its
   529	// LEXICAL binding — replacing module.exports.buildChangeProjection would not touch that call.
   530	// Every consumer therefore resolves the reference AT CALL TIME through current*(). Both
   531	// overrides default to null, so a configured run is byte-identical to its pre-seam form.
   532	let projectionBuilderOverride = null;
   533	function _setProjectionBuilder(fn) { projectionBuilderOverride = fn; }
   534	function currentProjectionBuilder() { return projectionBuilderOverride || buildChangeProjection; }
   535	let testRunnerOverride = null;
   536	function _setTestRunner(fn) { testRunnerOverride = fn; }
   537	// deliberately NOT exported — only verify() reads it; gate never runs a test command
   538	function currentTestRunner() { return testRunnerOverride || runTestCommand; }
   539	
   540	// ---- change scope (verify --change): requirement-block provenance over the projection ----
   541	// Collect every scenario occurrence of the projection ONCE, tagging each pair with whether
   542	// it lives inside a change-scoped block (produced/renamed by ADDED/MODIFIED/RENAMED; blocks
   543	// whose final projected state is deprecated are excluded — they are stripped before scanning).
   544	function collectChangePairs(texts, deltaOps) {
   545	  const pairs = [];                       // [label, title, scoped]
   546	  const requirements = [];                // [{file, name, operations}]
   547	  for (const [label, text] of texts) {
   548	    const clean = am.stripDeprecatedBlocks(stripFences(text));
   549	    const ops = deltaOps.get(label);
   550	    if (!ops) {
   551	      const sub = [];
   552	      scanTitles(clean, label, sub, true);
   553	      pairs.push(...sub.map(([l, t]) => [l, t, false]));
   554	      continue;
   555	    }
   556	    const removed = new Set(ops.removed);
   557	    const renamedNew = ops.renamedPairs.map(([, n]) => n);
   558	    const scopeNames = new Set([...ops.added, ...ops.modified, ...renamedNew].filter((n) => !removed.has(n)));
   559	    const { map } = am.parseRequirementsStrict(clean);
   560	    const firstBlock = clean.search(/^###\s+Requirement:/m);
   561	    const preamble = firstBlock >= 0 ? clean.slice(0, firstBlock) : clean;
   562	    const sub = [];
   563	    scanTitles(preamble, label, sub, true);
   564	    pairs.push(...sub.map(([l, t]) => [l, t, false]));
   565	    for (const [name, block] of map) {
   566	      const scoped = scopeNames.has(name);
   567	      if (scoped) {
   568	        const operations = [];
   569	        if (renamedNew.includes(name)) operations.push('RENAMED');
   570	        if (ops.added.includes(name)) operations.push('ADDED');
   785	}
   786	
   787	// Fallback test command from apriori/process-config.md's `test-cmd` row (written by init --test-cmd)
   788	function configTestCmd(cwd) {
   789	  const { value, problem } = require('./config').getConfig(cwd, 'test-cmd');
   790	  if (problem) return { error: problem };
   791	  return value;
   792	}
   793	
   794	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori verify --specs <dir...> --test-cmd "<cmd>" [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]\n   or: apriori verify --change <name> --test-cmd "<cmd>" [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]\n(--test-cmd may be omitted when apriori/process-config.md has a test-cmd row;\n --id-pattern may be omitted when apriori/process-config.md has an id-pattern row)';
   795	
   796	function cli(argv) {
   797	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'verify', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   798	    flags: { '--specs': 'multi', '--change': 'value', '--test-cmd': 'value', '--id-pattern': 'value', '--cwd': 'value', '--json': 'flag' } }, (f) => {
   799	    const a = { specs: f['--specs'] || [], change: f['--change'] || null, testCmd: f['--test-cmd'] || null,
   800	      idPattern: ('--id-pattern' in f) ? f['--id-pattern'] : null,   // PRESENCE, not truthiness (SR-52 empty flag)
   801	      cwd: f['--cwd'] || '.', json: !!f['--json'] };
   802	    if (a.change && a.specs.length) {
   803	      console.error('verify: --specs cannot be combined with --change — the projection defines the spec set');
   804	      return 2;
   805	    }
   806	    if (!a.testCmd) {
   807	      const c = configTestCmd(a.cwd);                    // init --test-cmd persists it there
   808	      if (c && c.error) { console.error(`verify: ${c.error}`); return 2; }
   809	      a.testCmd = c;
   810	    }
   811	    if ((!a.specs.length && !a.change) || !a.testCmd) { console.error(USAGE); return 2; }
   812	    const run = verify(a);
   813	    if (run.projection)
   814	      for (const sfx of run.projection.unstampedMutations || []) console.error('warning: ' + am.unstampedWarning(sfx));
   815	    if (run.errors.length && !a.json)
   816	      for (const e of run.errors) console.error('error: ' + e);
   817	    console.log(a.json ? JSON.stringify(verifyJson(run), null, 2)
   818	                       : formatReport(run.verdict, run.results, run.fileCount, { duplicates: run.duplicates, errors: run.errors, stderr: run.stderr, storeReport: run.storeReport, vacuousNote: run.vacuousNote, modifiedIntegrity: run.modifiedIntegrity }));
   819	    if (run.errors.length) return 2;
   820	    return run.verdict.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0 ? 0 : 1;
   821	  });
   822	}

   242	
   243	**关于 GT-25（config 来源的 matcher 被终止 → gate ERROR）**：该场景的前提是**执行**场景匹配子进程，
   244	而匹配只在收集场景时发生（C1 的工作）。C1 被 skip 时不收集场景，故 GT-25 的运行期前提**不可达**，
   245	其判定保持原样、无需修改。M3 覆盖的是**解析/编译期**失败（进程内、廉价），与 GT-25 不是同一件事。
   246	
   247	**为什么 C1 被 skip 时仍要解析 id-pattern（M3）**：一个坏掉的 pattern 是**坏配置**，
   248	不是**缺配置**。容忍它会造出第二个 false-negative（"配置全坏但 gate 说 INCOMPLETE"），
   249	且会让 GT-24 的既有断言不再普遍成立。O4 要求七项检查的判据一字不动——保持 ERROR 正是履行它。
   250	
   251	**M3 为何不含「config 文件不可读」（r3 勘误）**：一份不可读的 `process-config.md`
   252	在 ④（测试命令来源判定）就已命中 **T4** 并以 ERROR/2 退出，根本走不到 ⑤ 的 id-pattern 解析。
   253	「T7 缺席 + config 不可读」是**不可构造**的组合，v3 把它写进 M3 与 AC-GD-13 是一个不可达的验收分支。
   254	该情形由 T4 / AC-GD-08 **完整拥有**。M3 保留的是那些能与 T7 共存的 **key 局部**问题
   255	（`id-pattern` 行自身冲突、值不可编译、flag 为空或不可编译）。
   256	
   257	**M4 为何按「builder 的 errors」判而不是按错误种类枚举（r3 勘误）**：
   258	`buildChangeProjection` 的失败面不止 merge conflict / delta 畸形 / CAS 偏移——
   259	`discoverDeltas()` 的 validation 失败（该 change 没有任何 delta 文件、change/specs/delta 路径逃逸等）
   260	同样可达，且既不属于前三类、也绝不该落进 M1。因此 M4 的判据统一为
   261	「**builder 返回非空 errors，或未产出可信 texts**」，覆盖其全部失败类别。
   262	
   263	---
   264	
   265	## 四、验收标准（每条可表达为「若…则…」）
   266	
   267	### gate 侧
   268	
   269	| ID | 若 | 则 |
   270	|---|---|---|
   271	| AC-GD-01 | T7（测试命令缺席），对一个 in-flight change 跑 `apriori gate --change X` | C1 状态为 `skipped`，其 detail 同时含「未执行」的事实与修复指引；C2..C7 各自产出真实状态 |
   272	| AC-GD-02 | 矩阵 M1 | `result: INCOMPLETE`，退出码 **3**；人类可读输出显式声明「未达成 PASS，因为 C1 未执行」 |
   273	| AC-GD-03 | 矩阵 M2 | `BLOCKED`，退出码 **1**——已确证的阻断优先于不完整 |
   274	| AC-GD-04 | 矩阵 M5 | 退出码 **2**，与本 change 前逐字节一致 |
   275	| AC-GD-05 | 矩阵 M4 | 退出码 **2**；C7 不得基于坏 projection 给出任何结论 |
   276	| AC-GD-06 | T7 且该 change 有**未打戳的 mutation delta** | C7 仍 `blocked` 并给出 `apriori stamp` 修复指引——证明 C7 消费的是**真实** projection |
   277	| AC-GD-07 | T2 / T3（`--test-cmd ""` 或仅空白） | ERROR，退出码 **2**；不回落 config、不降级 skipped。CLI 层按**存在性**传参，`runGate({testCmd:''})` 与 `runGate({testCmd:null})` 结果不同（§3.1b） |
   278	| AC-GD-08 | T4 / T5（config 不可读，或 `test-cmd` 行冲突） | ERROR，退出码 **2** |
   279	| AC-GD-08b | T7 的子情形：config 有 `test-cmd` 行但值为空/仅空白 | 归入**缺席**（同 AC-GD-01/02），**不是** ERROR——共享 parser 按契约已把它等同于「无该行」（`lib/config.js:67`）；本 change 不触碰 `lib/config.js` |
   280	| AC-GD-09 | T1 / T6（任一有效来源） | gate 的检查集合、每项 detail 文案、`--json` 键集与取值、退出码**与本 change 前完全一致**（回归护栏） |
   281	| AC-GD-10 | 任一情形加 `--json` | 输出是纯 JSON，键集恒为 `{change, stage, checks, result, blocked, errors}`（**不新增 `code` 字段**）；`result` 可取 `INCOMPLETE`，`checks[].status` 可取 `skipped`；进程退出码按 `PASS→0 / BLOCKED→1 / ERROR→2 / INCOMPLETE→3` 映射 |
   282	| AC-GD-11 | 矩阵 M8（已归档 change + T7） | C1 `skipped`、C7 `n/a`、C4 按归档态要求全行终态；退出码按 B2 优先级 |
   283	| AC-GD-12 | 矩阵 M7（hotfix bundle + T7） | 仍以 mapping m1 的拒绝退出（退出码 2 并指向 `apriori hotfix archive`） |
   284	| AC-GD-13 | 矩阵 M3 的**每一个**子情形（空 flag / 不可编译 flag / 不可编译的 `id-pattern` config 值 / `id-pattern` 行冲突），各自叠加 T7 | 退出码 **2**，与本 change 前一致；对**已归档** change 亦然（M3 优先于 M8）。「config 文件不可读」**不在**本条，它归 AC-GD-08（T4） |
   285	| AC-GD-13b | `runGate({testCmd: <非字符串>})`（数字 / 数组 / 对象 / 布尔） | ERROR，退出码 **2**，消息点明类型；**不得**把该值交给 `spawnSync` 去抛异常 |
   286	| AC-GD-14 | T7 的任一情形 | **测试进程一次都不被 spawn**——以 `_setTestRunner` 计数 seam 断言调用数为 **0**（`_setChildRunner` 只拦 ID matcher 子进程，用它断言此事是错的） |
   287	| AC-GD-15a | 静态检查 `lib/gate.js` 源码 | ① **否定面**：不出现 `discoverDeltas` / `buildProjection` 字样（gate 对 `archive-merge` 的既有依赖仅限 `CHANGE_NAME_RE` / `containsReal`，故不误伤）；② **肯定面**：gate 确实从 `./spec-runner` 引入并调用那个共享的 projection-only 入口 |
   288	| AC-GD-15b | 用 `_setProjectionBuilder` 包裹**唯一**的 projection-only 构建引用，然后分别跑「T7 路径的 gate」与「有测试命令的 `verify --change`」 | 两条路径都恰好经过**同一个** wrapper（身份可断言）；T7 路径下 builder 被调用 **1** 次、`_setTestRunner` 计数为 **0** 次 |
   289	| AC-GD-15c | 对 6 个 fixture——clean / 未打戳 mutation / delta 畸形 / CAS base 偏移 / merge conflict / **无 delta 文件（`discoverDeltas` 失败）**——分别走两条路径（T7 路径的 projection 由 15b 的 wrapper 捕获） | 两次得到的**完整** projection 对象（`modules` / `conflicts` / `unstampedMutations` / `notes` 全字段）**与** errors 数组逐项相等——成功与失败两类结果都比 |
   290

   315	
   650	      modifiedBlocks: b.modifiedBlocks, titleId };
   651	  } else {
   652	    collected = collectScenarios(opts.specs, idRe, matcher);
   653	    if (collected.failure) return errorsRun([matcherFailureMsg(collected.failure)], projection);
   654	  }
   655	  const { byId, unidentified, files, missingTargets, duplicates } = collected;
   656	  const exec = currentTestRunner()(opts.testCmd, cwd);
   657	  const parsed = parseTap(exec.out, idRe, matcher);
   658	  if (parsed.matchFailure) {
   659	    const run = errorsRun([matcherFailureMsg(parsed.matchFailure)], projection);
   660	    run.exec = { status: exec.status, signal: exec.signal, error: exec.error };
   661	    run.stderr = exec.stderr || '';
   662	    return run;
   663	  }
   664	  const { results, untagged, untaggedFails, unattributedFailures, bailout, plans, points, dupNumbers, tapProblems } = parsed;
   665	  const verdict = evaluate(byId, unidentified, results, unattributedFailures);
   666	  const failCount = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.fail, 0) + untaggedFails;
   667	  const run = {
   668	    verdict, results, fileCount: files.length, failCount,
   669	    scenarioCount: byId.size + unidentified.length,
   670	    missingTargets, duplicates, bailout, plans, points, dupNumbers,
   671	    noTap: zeroTapParsed(exec.out, results, untagged, unattributedFailures)
   672	      || (!exec.out.trim() && looksLikeTap(exec.stderr)),  // TAP went to stderr — same zero-TAP cure (2>&1)
   673	    tapProblems,
   674	    stderr: exec.stderr || '',
   675	    exec: { status: exec.status, signal: exec.signal, error: exec.error },
   676	  };
   677	  if (projection) run.projection = projection;
   678	  run.errors = infraErrors(run);
   679	  // a named scope judges that set and nothing else (SR-69..72); an untrustworthy run
   680	  // yields no scoped verdict at all rather than a satisfiable-looking one
   681	  if (opts.scope) run.scopedVerdict = run.errors.length ? null : scopedEvaluate(byId, results, opts.scope);
   682	  // change-scoped verdict split (SR-56..62): only on trustworthy --change runs; every
   683	  // ERROR class leaves the run without storeReport/changeScope (absent, never null)
   684	  if (changeMeta && run.errors.length === 0) {
   685	    applyChangeScope(run, changeMeta);
   686	    // the modified-block integrity report (informative; ALWAYS present on GREEN/GAPS)
   687	    const idOf = (title) => { const v = changeMeta.titleId.get(title); return v === undefined ? null : v; };
   688	    const entries = [];
   689	    for (const [file, list] of (changeMeta.modifiedBlocks || new Map()))
   690	      for (const { name, oldBlock, newBlock } of list)
   691	        entries.push({ file, name, ...am.compareModifiedBlock(oldBlock, newBlock, idOf) });
   692	    entries.sort((a, b2) => (a.file === b2.file ? (a.name < b2.name ? -1 : 1) : (a.file < b2.file ? -1 : 1)));
   693	    run.modifiedIntegrity = entries;
   694	  }
   695	  return run;
   696	}
   697	
   698	// Split a trustworthy --change run into the change verdict + the informative store report.
   699	// Fail-closed on failure signals: only a red BOUND to an out-of-scope projection scenario
   700	// is non-blocking; unattributed failures and FAILING true orphans block (no provenance).
   701	function applyChangeScope(run, meta) {
   702	  const full = run.verdict;                                  // whole-projection evaluation
   703	  const { pairs, ids, requirements } = meta;
   704	  const occCount = new Map();
   705	  ids.forEach((id) => { if (id) occCount.set(id, (occCount.get(id) || 0) + 1); });
   706	  const scopedIdSet = new Set();
   707	  const scopedUnidentified = [];
   708	  const allFiles = new Map();                                // id → Set(files), EVERY occurrence
   709	  pairs.forEach(([label, title, scoped], i) => {
   710	    const id = ids[i];
   711	    if (id) {
   712	      if (!allFiles.has(id)) allFiles.set(id, new Set());
   713	      allFiles.get(id).add(label);
   714	    }
   715	    if (!scoped) return;
   716	    if (id) scopedIdSet.add(id);
   717	    else scopedUnidentified.push([label, title]);
   718	  });
   719	  const scopedIds = [...scopedIdSet].sort();
   720	  const boundGreen = [], boundRed = [], unbound = [];
   721	  for (const id of scopedIds) {
   722	    const r = run.results.get(id);
   723	    if (!r || (r.pass === 0 && r.fail === 0)) unbound.push(id);
   724	    else if (r.fail === 0) boundGreen.push(id);
   725	    else boundRed.push(id);
   726	  }
   727	  // duplicate provenance carries EVERY occurrence's file — the out-of-scope colliding
   728	  // file is exactly what explains the ambiguity (SR-13 convention)
   729	  const scopedDup = scopedIds.filter((id) => occCount.get(id) > 1)
   730	    .map((id) => ({ id, files: [...allFiles.get(id)] }));
   731	  // a failing orphan blocks unless a sibling active change declares its ID (then it is
   732	  // provably that change's business — still visible in storeReport.orphan)
   733	  const siblingIds = meta.siblingIds || new Set();
   734	  const failingOrphans = full.orphan.filter((id) => (run.results.get(id) || {}).fail > 0 && !siblingIds.has(id));
   735	  const unattributed = full.unattributed || [];
   736	  const clean = !boundRed.length && !unbound.length && !scopedUnidentified.length &&
   737	    !scopedDup.length && !failingOrphans.length && !unattributed.length;
   738	  run.storeReport = {
   739	    boundGreen: full.boundGreen.length,
   740	    boundRed: full.boundRed, unbound: full.unbound, orphan: full.orphan,
   741	    unidentified: full.unidentified,
   742	    unattributedFailures: { count: unattributed.length, lines: unattributed },
   743	    duplicates: run.duplicates,
   744	  };
   745	  run.changeScope = { requirements, scenarioIds: scopedIds };
   746	  run.verdict = { boundGreen, boundRed, unbound, orphan: failingOrphans,
   747	    unidentified: scopedUnidentified, unattributed, clean };
   748	  run.duplicates = scopedDup;
   749	  if (scopedIds.length === 0 && scopedUnidentified.length === 0)
   750	    run.vacuousNote = vacuousNoteFor(meta.deltaOps);
   751	}
   752	
   753	// machine-consumable shape for a verify run
   754	function verifyJson(run) {
   755	  const { verdict: v, results } = run;
   756	  const clean = v.clean && run.duplicates.length === 0 && run.errors.length === 0;
   757	  const json = projectionless(run, clean);
   758	  if (run.projection) json.projection = run.projection;   // ONLY --change runs carry this field
   759	  if (run.storeReport) {                                  // ONLY non-ERROR --change runs (absent otherwise)
   760	    json.storeReport = { ...run.storeReport,
   761	      unidentified: run.storeReport.unidentified.map(([file, title]) => ({ file, title })) };
   762	    json.changeScope = run.changeScope;
   763	    json.modifiedIntegrity = run.modifiedIntegrity;
   764	  }
   765	  return json;
   766	}
   767	
   768	function projectionless(run, clean) {
   769	  const { verdict: v, results } = run;
   770	  return {
   771	    clean,
   772	    result: run.errors.length ? 'ERROR' : (clean ? 'GREEN' : 'GAPS'),
   773	    errors: run.errors,
   774	    specFiles: run.fileCount,
   775	    exec: run.exec,
   776	    duplicates: run.duplicates,
   777	    boundGreen: v.boundGreen.map((id) => ({ id, ...results.get(id) })),
   778	    boundRed: v.boundRed.map((id) => ({ id, ...results.get(id) })),
   779	    unbound: v.unbound,
   780	    orphan: v.orphan.map((id) => ({ id, ...results.get(id) })),
   781	    unidentified: v.unidentified.map(([file, title]) => ({ file, title })),
   782	    unattributedFailures: { count: (v.unattributed || []).length, lines: v.unattributed || [] },
   783	    stderr: run.stderr || '',
   784	  };
   785	}
   786	
   787	// Fallback test command from apriori/process-config.md's `test-cmd` row (written by init --test-cmd)
   788	function configTestCmd(cwd) {
   789	  const { value, problem } = require('./config').getConfig(cwd, 'test-cmd');
   790	  if (problem) return { error: problem };
   390	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori gate --change <name> [--test-cmd "<cmd>"] [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json] [--no-cas]';
   391	
   392	function cli(argv) {
   393	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'gate', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   394	    flags: { '--change': 'value', '--test-cmd': 'value', '--id-pattern': 'value', '--cwd': 'value', '--json': 'flag', '--no-cas': 'flag' },
   395	    jsonError: (m) => JSON.stringify({ change: null, stage: null, checks: [], result: 'ERROR', blocked: 0, errors: [m] }, null, 2) }, (f) => {
   396	    const a = { change: f['--change'] || null, testCmd: ('--test-cmd' in f) ? f['--test-cmd'] : null,   // PRESENCE, not truthiness — an empty flag is an error, never a config fallback
   397	      idPattern: ('--id-pattern' in f) ? f['--id-pattern'] : null,   // PRESENCE, not truthiness (GT-24 empty flag)
   398	      cwd: f['--cwd'] || process.cwd(), json: !!f['--json'], noCas: !!f['--no-cas'] };
   399	    const res = runGate(a);
   400	    if (a.json) { console.log(JSON.stringify(toJson(res), null, 2)); return res.code; }
   401	    for (const e of res.errors) console.error('gate: ' + e);
   402	    const mark = { pass: '✓', blocked: '✗', 'n/a': '–', skipped: '○' };
   403	    for (const c of res.checks) console.log(`${mark[c.status]} ${c.id} ${c.status === 'blocked' ? 'BLOCKED — ' : ''}${c.detail}`);
   404	    if (res.code === 0) console.log(`\nGATE: PASS — ${CAVEAT}`);
   405	    else if (res.code === 1) console.log(`\nGATE: BLOCKED (${res.blocked} item(s))`);
   406	    else if (res.code === 3) console.log('\nGATE: INCOMPLETE — C1 did not run; PASS was not reached');
   407	    return res.code;
   408	  });
   409	}

@@ -2,6 +2,40 @@
 
 All notable changes to `apriori-cli`. Versions follow semver; the stability promise: CLI surface & flags, `--json` shapes, the delta format and the flow-state schema only break in a major.
 
+## Unreleased · gate degrades the check it cannot run — `INCOMPLETE`, exit code 3
+
+**Exit-code semantics extended.** `apriori gate` gains a fourth outcome: `GATE: INCOMPLETE`
+with **exit code 3**, and `checks[].status` gains `skipped`. If your CI treats any non-zero
+exit as failure, an INCOMPLETE gate will now fail the job — that is the intended reading: a
+gate that could not run its binding check has not passed. The `--json` key set is unchanged
+(`{change, stage, checks, result, blocked, errors}` — still no `code` field); only the value
+domains of `result` and `checks[].status` grew, and `blocked` still counts blocked checks
+only, never skipped ones.
+
+**Why.** A project whose `apriori/process-config.md` lacked a `test-cmd` row got exit 2 from
+`apriori gate` before a single check ran — so C2 (tasks all checked) and C4 (ledger terminal)
+never executed either, though neither needs a test command. In a real brownfield project that
+meant the gate ran zero times across an entire change's life, while 45 unchecked tasks and
+zero consistency reviews sailed through. Now a missing test command skips C1 alone: the other
+six checks run and report real conclusions, and the aggregate says so. A *broken* test-command
+source (conflicting or unreadable config, an empty or whitespace-only `--test-cmd`, a
+non-string value through the API) stays exit 2 — broken is not absent. `--test-cmd` is now
+judged by PRESENCE, matching `--id-pattern`: an empty flag is an error, never a config
+fallback. A confirmed block still outranks a skip.
+
+**`apriori doctor` stops calling that project healthy.** D5 reported `n/a` when no test
+command was configured, and `n/a` is not a finding — so doctor printed `DOCTOR: HEALTHY` about
+a project whose gate was dead. It is now a finding naming the consequence, with the
+`process-config.md` fix. An explicit `--no-run` is still `n/a`: a deliberate skip is not a
+defect, and that branch now sorts ahead of the missing-command one.
+
+**Internals.** `lib/spec-runner.js` exports the change-projection builder plus a call-time
+resolver and two test-only seams, so gate's skipped-C1 path provably shares verify's one
+projection implementation rather than growing a second — a divergence there would have
+silently un-blocked C7.
+
+391 tests.
+
 ## Unreleased · the SR-64 byte-golden guard becomes platform-correct
 
 The `--specs` byte goldens were captured on POSIX and compared byte-for-byte on every

@@ -21,6 +21,7 @@ A detected AI tool's rules file no longer points at `apriori/runbook.md`, or its
 - *produced no output / not TAP*: your runner uses a human reporter. Node: add `--test-reporter=tap`; pytest: `pytest --tap-stream` (plugin `pytest-tap`).
 - *TAP stream truncated or malformed*: a version/plan line appeared but zero result lines — the run died mid-stream.
 - *exit N unexplained by TAP*: the command failed without a corresponding `not ok` — often a crashing test file or a misconfigured runner.
+- *no test command configured*: this is a FINDING, not a neutral note — without one, `apriori gate` cannot run C1 (its binding check) at all, and gate reports `GATE: INCOMPLETE` (exit 3) for every change you run it on. Fix: add a `test-cmd` row to `apriori/process-config.md` (that file is yours — the agent only reads it), or pass `--test-cmd` per invocation. An explicit `--no-run` stays not-applicable: a deliberate skip is not a defect.
 - Red tests are NOT doctor findings — that is `verify`'s business.

+// The same shape as checkBinding, for the path where no test command exists. C1 cannot run,
+// but everything C1 was ALSO producing for its neighbours still can: C7 needs the projection,
+// and a broken id-pattern is still a broken config. Nothing here spawns a process.
+function checkBindingSkipped(cwd, name, stage, idPattern) {
+  // a broken pattern is a broken CONFIG, not an absent one — it stays an evaluation error
+  // (GT-24 keeps holding). Compile-checking is in-process; no scenario is ever MATCHED here,
+  // so no matcher child is spawned and GT-25's runtime precondition cannot arise.
+  const idp = resolveIdPattern(cwd, idPattern);
+  if (idp.error) return { infra: [idp.error] };
+  const detail = 'skipped — no test command (pass --test-cmd or add a test-cmd row to '
+    + 'apriori/process-config.md); the binding check did not run';
+  const c1 = { id: 'C1', status: 'skipped', detail };
+  // archived: the deltas are already merged and C7 is n/a — building a projection could only
+  // manufacture a false block, so it is never built at all.
+  if (stage === 'archived') return { check: c1, projection: null };
+  const b = sr.currentProjectionBuilder()(name, cwd);
+  if (b.errors && b.errors.length) return { infra: b.errors };
+  // fail closed: no trustworthy texts is untrustworthy even when nothing said why. Reporting
+  // it with an empty errors list would be an ERROR that cannot explain itself.
+  if (!b.texts) return { infra: ['projection produced no trustworthy texts and reported no error — refusing to judge'] };
+  return { check: c1, projection: b.projection };
+}
+
+// The test command has three fates, and "broken" is not "absent". A flag that is present but
+// empty (or all whitespace) is an operator error in THIS invocation and must never fall back
+// to the config; an empty config VALUE, by contrast, was already normalised to "no such row"
+// by the shared reader (config.js), so gate sees an absence and treats it as one.
+function resolveTestCmd(opts, cwd) {
+  const flag = opts.testCmd;
+  if (flag !== undefined && flag !== null) {
+    if (typeof flag !== 'string') return { kind: 'error', error: `--test-cmd must be a string (got ${Array.isArray(flag) ? 'array' : typeof flag})` };
+    if (flag.trim() === '') return { kind: 'error', error: 'empty --test-cmd — pass a command or omit the flag' };
+    return { kind: 'ok', value: flag };
+  }
+  const c = configTestCmd(cwd);
+  if (c && c.error) return { kind: 'error', error: `verify: ${c.error}` };
+  if (c) return { kind: 'ok', value: c };
+  return { kind: 'absent' };
+}
+

+// SECOND consumer (gate's skipped-C1 path) can be proven to share this file's one projection

     1	'use strict';
     2	/*
     3	 * spec-runner — bind spec scenario IDs to test runs, report per-scenario red/green.
     4	 * VISION endgame, weak form: "spec IS the test suite". Zero deps — pure Node stdlib.
     5	 * Coupling surface is tiny and language-agnostic: markdown scenarios + a TAP-emitting test command.
     6	 *
     7	 * FAIL-CLOSED: a deterministic gate must refuse to say GREEN when its inputs are missing,
     8	 * its instrument misbehaved, or its evidence is ambiguous. Vacuous success is a bug.
     9	 * Exit codes: 0 = GREEN · 1 = spec/test gaps · 2 = invocation or infrastructure error.
    10	 */
    11	const fs = require('fs');
    12	const path = require('path');
    13	const { spawnSync } = require('child_process');
    14	// merge-family helpers shared with archive (3.1 projection); archive-merge never requires spec-runner
    15	const am = require('./archive-merge');
    16	const { withStrict } = require('./args');
    17	// DEFAULT_ID lives in config (the zero-dep resolution home) and is re-exported here unchanged
    18	const { DEFAULT_ID, resolveIdPattern, sanitizeMsg } = require('./config');
    19	const SCENARIO_RE = /^####\s+Scenario:\s+(.*)$/gm;
    20	// TAP result line, directive split off separately (# SKIP / # TODO)
    21	const TAP_RE = /^(ok|not ok)\s+\d+\s+-\s+(.*)$/gm;
    22	
    23	// Leading scenario/test ID. The match must end at a word boundary: `XX-01b` is NOT `XX-01` —
    24	// silently truncating would merge two different scenarios into one binding.
    25	function leadId(text, idRe) {
    26	  const m = text.trim().match(idRe);
    27	  if (!m || m.index !== 0) return null;
    28	  const next = text.trim()[m[0].length];
    29	  if (next !== undefined && /[A-Za-z0-9_]/.test(next)) return null;
    30	  return m[0];
    31	}
    32	
    33	function stripFences(text) { return text.replace(/```[\s\S]*?```/g, ''); }
    34	
    35	// ---- the id matcher: one recognition contract, two execution channels -------------------
    36	// A config-sourced pattern is repository input that CI consumes automatically; its every
    37	// actual application runs inside a terminable child (SIGKILL on budget) so a catastrophic
    38	// pattern can never hang the CLI. Flag/default origins are operator/built-in trust: inline.
    39	const CHILD_PATH = path.join(__dirname, 'id-match-child.js');
    40	// the child's spawn contract, exported so tests assert the budget DETERMINISTICALLY
    41	// (a wall-clock window cannot prove the 2000ms figure)
    42	const CHILD_SPAWN_OPTS = Object.freeze({ timeout: 2000, killSignal: 'SIGKILL', shell: false });
    43	let childRunnerOverride = null;                 // test seam — SR-55 injects failures here
    44	function _setChildRunner(fn) { childRunnerOverride = fn; }
    45	function defaultChildRunner(payload) {
    46	  const r = spawnSync(process.execPath, [CHILD_PATH], {
    47	    input: payload, ...CHILD_SPAWN_OPTS,
    48	    encoding: 'utf8', maxBuffer: 8 * 1024 * 1024,
    49	  });
    50	  return { status: r.status, signal: r.signal || null, stdout: r.stdout || '', error: r.error || null };
    51	}
    52	// resolved = resolveIdPattern's success shape → { batch(texts) → {ids:(string|null)[]} | {failure} }
    53	function makeIdMatcher(resolved) {
    54	  if (resolved.origin !== 'config') {
    55	    const re = new RegExp(resolved.source);
    56	    return { batch: (texts) => ({ ids: texts.map((t) => leadId(t, re)) }) };
    57	  }
    58	  return { batch: (texts) => {
    59	    const runner = childRunnerOverride || defaultChildRunner;
    60	    let r;
    61	    try { r = runner(JSON.stringify({ pattern: resolved.source, texts })); }
    62	    catch (e) { return { failure: `spawn-error: ${String(e.message || e)}` }; }
    63	    // timeout FIRST: a real spawnSync timeout carries ETIMEDOUT and/or the kill signal —
    64	    // classifying by r.error first would misreport the termination as a spawn error
    65	    if ((r.error && r.error.code === 'ETIMEDOUT') || r.signal === 'SIGKILL')
    66	      return { failure: 'timeout (budget exceeded, child terminated)' };
    67	    if (r.error) return { failure: `spawn-error: ${String(r.error.message || r.error)}` };
    68	    if (r.signal) return { failure: `signal ${r.signal}` };
    69	    if (r.status !== 0) return { failure: `non-zero-exit (${r.status})` };
    70	    let parsed;
    71	    try { parsed = JSON.parse(r.stdout); } catch { return { failure: 'malformed-output (not JSON)' }; }
    72	    // strict success shape: exactly one JSON document whose ONLY key is a well-formed ids array
    73	    if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object' || Array.isArray(parsed) ||
    74	        Object.keys(parsed).length !== 1 || !Array.isArray(parsed.ids) ||
    75	        parsed.ids.length !== texts.length ||
    76	        parsed.ids.some((x) => x !== null && typeof x !== 'string'))
    77	      return { failure: 'malformed-output (bad shape)' };
    78	    return { ids: parsed.ids };
    79	  } };
    80	}
    81	// the one sanitized failure text every consumer surfaces for a config-origin matcher failure
    82	function matcherFailureMsg(failure) {
    83	  return sanitizeMsg(`process-config id-pattern matching failed (${failure})`);
    84	}
    85	const inlineMatcher = (idRe) => makeIdMatcher({ source: idRe.source, origin: 'default' });
    86	
    87	function mdFiles(target) {
    88	  if (!fs.existsSync(target)) return [];
    89	  const st = fs.statSync(target);
    90	  if (st.isFile()) return [target];
    91	  const out = [];
    92	  for (const e of fs.readdirSync(target, { withFileTypes: true })) {
    93	    const p = path.join(target, e.name);
    94	    if (e.isDirectory()) out.push(...mdFiles(p));
    95	    else if (e.name.endsWith('.md')) out.push(p);
    96	  }
    97	  return out.sort();
    98	}

   100	// Shared scenario scan over one text — STRUCTURE ONLY (no ID extraction; that is the
   101	// matcher's job so config-origin patterns stay inside the terminable channel).
   102	// Fenced content is documentation; scenarios inside DEPRECATED requirement blocks
   103	// (the marker REMOVED injects) are excluded in every form — they are neither demanded
   104	// nor listed, and their lingering tests become ORPHAN.
   105	function scanTitles(text, label, pairs, preClean) {
   106	  const clean = preClean ? text : am.stripDeprecatedBlocks(stripFences(text));
   107	  let m;
   108	  SCENARIO_RE.lastIndex = 0;
   109	  while ((m = SCENARIO_RE.exec(clean)) !== null) pairs.push([label, m[1]]);
   110	}
   111	
   112	// One batch over tagged pairs ([label,title,scoped]) plus sibling titles (appended to the
   113	// SAME batch — never a third matcher call) → { acc, ids, siblingIds } or { failure }.
   114	function makeThenBind(pairs, matcher, siblingTitles = [], integrityTitles = []) {
   115	  const titles = [...pairs.map((p) => p[1]), ...siblingTitles, ...integrityTitles];
   116	  const res = matcher.batch(titles);
   117	  if (res.failure) return { failure: res.failure };
   118	  const acc = { byId: new Map(), idFiles: new Map(), unidentified: [] };
   119	  pairs.forEach(([label, title], i) => {
   120	    const id = res.ids[i];

    16	function mkProject(files) {
    17	  const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'apriori-gate-'));
    18	  for (const [rel, content] of Object.entries(files)) {
    19	    const p = path.join(root, rel);
    20	    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(p), { recursive: true });
    21	    fs.writeFileSync(p, content);
    22	  }
    23	  return root;
    24	}

   121	  assert.strictEqual(gate.runGate({ cwd: root, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK }).checks.find((x) => x.id === 'C5').status, 'pass');
   122	  // symlinked doc-glob match blocks (where the platform allows symlinks)
   123	  let canSymlink = true;
   124	  try { fs.symlinkSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c/review/req-review-v1.md'), path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c/review/c-linked.md')); }
   125	  catch { canSymlink = false; }
   126	  if (canSymlink) {
   127	    r = gate.runGate({ cwd: root, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK });
   128	    c5 = r.checks.find((x) => x.id === 'C5');
   129	    assert.strictEqual(c5.status, 'blocked');
   130	    assert.match(c5.detail, /c-linked/);
   131	    fs.rmSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c/review/c-linked.md'));
   132	    // a SYMLINKED raw is not evidence: replace the real raw with a symlink → blocked again
   133	    fs.renameSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt'), path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c/review/elsewhere.txt'));
   134	    fs.symlinkSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c/review/elsewhere.txt'), path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt'));
   135	    r = gate.runGate({ cwd: root, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK });
   136	    assert.strictEqual(r.checks.find((x) => x.id === 'C5').status, 'blocked');
   137	    // a DANGLING review/ symlink is a defect, not absence — C4 and C5 both block, in-flight…
   138	    const dang = healthy();
   139	    fs.rmSync(path.join(dang, 'apriori/changes/c/review'), { recursive: true });
   140	    fs.symlinkSync(path.join(dang, 'no-such-target'), path.join(dang, 'apriori/changes/c/review'));
   141	    r = gate.runGate({ cwd: dang, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK });
   142	    for (const id of ['C4', 'C5']) {
   143	      const c = r.checks.find((x) => x.id === id);
   144	      assert.strictEqual(c.status, 'blocked', `${id}: ${c.detail}`);
   145	      assert.match(c.detail, /symlink/);
   146	    }
   147	    // …and archived
   148	    const darch = mkProject({
   149	      'apriori/specs/kv/spec.md': STORE + '\n### Requirement: Beta\n\n#### Scenario: XB-01 new\n- t\n',
   150	      'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1200-c/flow-state.md': FLOW('c'),
   151	      'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1200-c/tasks.md': '- [x] T1\n',

   153	    });

   333	test('GT-12 gate is read-only', () => {
   334	  const root = healthy();
   335	  const snap = () => {
   336	    const out = [];
   337	    const walk = (d) => { for (const e of fs.readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true })) { const p = path.join(d, e.name); if (e.isDirectory()) walk(p); else out.push(p + ':' + fs.statSync(p).size + ':' + fs.statSync(p).mtimeMs); } };
   338	    walk(root);
   339	    return out.sort().join('\n');
   340	  };
   341	  const before = snap();
   342	  gate.runGate({ cwd: root, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK });
   343	  gate.runGate({ cwd: root, change: 'nope', testCmd: TAP_OK });
   344	  assert.strictEqual(snap(), before);

   347	// ---- ledger-states (GT-13..15): C4 speaks the terminal-state vocabulary ----
   348	
   349	const LEDGER_HDR = '| ID | Issue | Risk | Round found | Status |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n';
   350	const ledgerWith = (...rows) => LEDGER_HDR + rows.map((r) => `| ${r[0]} | i | low | 1 | ${r[1]} |\n`).join('');
   351	// FLOW plus an extra gates: entry line (the human waive record)
   352	const FLOW_G = (name, extra) => FLOW(name) + (extra ? `  - ${extra}\n` : '');
   353	
   354	function archProject(ledger, flowExtra) {
   355	  return mkProject({
   356	    'apriori/specs/kv/spec.md': STORE + '\n### Requirement: Beta\n\n#### Scenario: XB-01 new\n- t\n',
   357	    'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1200-c/flow-state.md': FLOW_G('c', flowExtra),
   358	    'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1200-c/tasks.md': '- [x] T1\n',
   359	    'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1200-c/specs/kv/spec.md': DELTA,
   360	    'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1200-c/review/issues.md': ledger,
   361	  });
   362	}
   363	const c4Of = (root) => gate.runGate({ cwd: root, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK }).checks.find((x) => x.id === 'C4');

   365	test('GT-13 archived ledgers must be terminal', () => {
   366	  // fixed at archived stage → blocked with the reviewer-verify cure
   367	  const f = c4Of(archProject(ledgerWith(['Q-1', 'fixed (v2)'])));
   368	  assert.strictEqual(f.status, 'blocked');
   369	  assert.match(f.detail, /reviewer must verify|never verified/);
   370	  // plain reasoned rejected → blocked with the concurrence cure
   371	  const r = c4Of(archProject(ledgerWith(['Q-1', 'rejected — cosmetic, out of scope'])));
   372	  assert.strictEqual(r.status, 'blocked');
   373	  assert.match(r.detail, /rejected-verified|reviewer concurrence/);
   374	  // unknown status → blocked naming the vocabulary
   375	  const u = c4Of(archProject(ledgerWith(['Q-1', 'done'])));
   376	  assert.strictEqual(u.status, 'blocked');
   377	  assert.match(u.detail, /vocabulary/);
   378	  // all-terminal → pass (waived backed by a gates: entry naming the ID)
   379	  const ok = c4Of(archProject(
   380	    ledgerWith(['Q-1', 'verified'],
   381	               ['Q-2', 'rejected-verified — cosmetic; reviewer concurred (review-v2)'],
   382	               ['Q-3', 'waived — owner accepts the perf risk'],
   383	               ['Q-4', 'advisory-acked']),
   384	    '2026-07-12T01:00 gate: owner waived Q-3 (perf risk accepted for this release)'));
   385	  assert.strictEqual(ok.status, 'pass', ok.detail);
   386	});

   388	test('GT-14 waives belong to humans, unknown states belong to nobody', () => {
   389	  // in-flight: waived without any gates: evidence → blocked
   390	  const root1 = healthy();
   391	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root1, 'apriori/changes/c/review/issues.md'), ledgerWith(['Q-1', 'waived — accepted']));
   392	  const w1 = c4Of(root1);
   393	  assert.strictEqual(w1.status, 'blocked');
   394	  assert.match(w1.detail, /gates: entry/);
   395	  // same row passes once the human decision is recorded in gates:
   396	  const root2 = healthy();
   397	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root2, 'apriori/changes/c/review/issues.md'), ledgerWith(['Q-1', 'waived — accepted']));
   398	  fs.appendFileSync(path.join(root2, 'apriori/changes/c/flow-state.md'),
   399	    '  - 2026-07-12T01:00 owner waived Q-1: risk accepted\n');
   400	  assert.strictEqual(c4Of(root2).status, 'pass');
   401	  // exact ID token: an entry waiving Q-10 never satisfies row Q-1
   402	  const root3 = healthy();
   403	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root3, 'apriori/changes/c/review/issues.md'), ledgerWith(['Q-1', 'waived — accepted']));
   404	  fs.appendFileSync(path.join(root3, 'apriori/changes/c/flow-state.md'),
   405	    '  - 2026-07-12T01:00 owner waived Q-10: a different row\n');
   406	  assert.strictEqual(c4Of(root3).status, 'blocked');
   407	  // ID in one entry + 'waived' in another entry never passes (same-entry rule)
   408	  const root4 = healthy();
   409	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root4, 'apriori/changes/c/review/issues.md'), ledgerWith(['Q-1', 'waived — accepted']));
   410	  fs.appendFileSync(path.join(root4, 'apriori/changes/c/flow-state.md'),
   411	    '  - 2026-07-12T01:00 note: Q-1 discussed\n  - 2026-07-12T01:01 something else waived here\n');
   412	  assert.strictEqual(c4Of(root4).status, 'blocked');
   413	  // unknown status blocks IN-FLIGHT too; reasonless terminals block
   414	  const root5 = healthy();
   415	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root5, 'apriori/changes/c/review/issues.md'), ledgerWith(['Q-1', 'verifed']));
   416	  assert.strictEqual(c4Of(root5).status, 'blocked');
   417	  const root6 = healthy();
   418	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root6, 'apriori/changes/c/review/issues.md'), ledgerWith(['Q-1', 'rejected-verified']));
   419	  assert.strictEqual(c4Of(root6).status, 'blocked');
   420	  // in-flight fixed and reasoned rejected still pass (the loop is running)
   421	  const root7 = healthy();
   422	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root7, 'apriori/changes/c/review/issues.md'),
   423	    ledgerWith(['Q-1', 'fixed (v2)'], ['Q-2', 'rejected — cosmetic, out of scope']));
   424	  assert.strictEqual(c4Of(root7).status, 'pass');
   425	});

   427	test('GT-15 every archived ledger in this repo parses legal and terminal', () => {
   428	  const archRoot = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'apriori', 'changes', 'archive');
   429	  if (!fs.existsSync(archRoot)) return;                     // corpus is local-only
   430	  const { classifyStatus } = gate;
   431	  assert.strictEqual(typeof classifyStatus, 'function');
   432	  for (const d of fs.readdirSync(archRoot)) {
   433	    const name = d.replace(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T\d{4}-/, '');
   434	    const lp = path.join(archRoot, d, 'review', 'issues.md');
   435	    if (!fs.existsSync(lp)) continue;
   436	    const { parseLedger } = require('../lib/status');
   437	    for (const row of parseLedger(fs.readFileSync(lp, 'utf8'))) {
   438	      const c = classifyStatus(row.status);
   439	      assert.ok(c.legal, `${name} ${row.id}: illegal status '${row.status}'`);
   440	      assert.ok(c.terminal, `${name} ${row.id}: non-terminal archived status '${row.status}'`);
   441	      if (c.needsReason) assert.ok(c.hasReason, `${name} ${row.id}: reasonless '${row.status}'`);
   442	    }
   443	  }
   444	});

    15	function proj(config) {

   104	test('CF-08 odd backslash runs keep the pipe in the value', () => {

   106	  assert.strictEqual(values.get('id-pattern'), '(AC|BR)-\\d+');

   113	  assert.strictEqual(two.values.get('k'), 'a\\\\');            // value ends with two literal backslashes
   114	  const four = parseConfig('| k | a\\\\\\\\|b |\n');           // raw: a\\\\|b — even (4), separator
   115	  assert.strictEqual(four.values.get('k'), 'a\\\\\\\\');

## MODIFIED Requirements

### Requirement: doctor diagnoses the project-apriori seam
`apriori doctor` SHALL run eight read-only checks (D1 Node floor (≥22) · D2 init scaffold · D3 runbook freshness · D4 tool pointers · D5 TAP probe · D6 store health · D7 changes overview · D8 legacy 3.x layout roots), each reporting ok/finding/not-applicable with a fix hint on findings, and SHALL encode the aggregate in its exit code: 0 = zero findings (`DOCTOR: HEALTHY`), 1 = ≥1 finding (`DOCTOR: <n> finding(s)`), 2 = unusable (Node below floor, `apriori/` missing entirely, positional-arg usage error) — printing whatever checks already ran. Doctor never repairs and never writes; its ONLY side effect is D5 executing the project's test command once, removed by `--no-run`.

#### Scenario: DR-01 a healthy initialized project reports HEALTHY
- WHEN every applicable check passes on a freshly initialized project with a valid TAP test command
- THEN each check reports ok or not-applicable, the final line is `DOCTOR: HEALTHY`, and the exit code is 0

#### Scenario: DR-02 an uninitialized project is unusable, with guidance
- WHEN `apriori/` does not exist under the target root
- THEN doctor exits 2, the message names `apriori init`, and `--json` reports `result: "UNUSABLE"` with the already-run checks (D1) listed

#### Scenario: DR-03 init-scaffold gaps are findings with their fixer
- WHEN `apriori/runbook.md`, `apriori/specs/`, `apriori/.gitignore` (with its `tmp/` line), or the `apriori/tmp/` dir is missing
- THEN D2 emits ONE check entry PER GAP (each with id `D2`, status finding, naming the file and its fixer `apriori init` / `apriori update`) — the aggregate `findings` count equals the number of finding-status entries; a missing `apriori/process-config.md` is not-applicable (optional, defaults apply), never a finding

#### Scenario: DR-04 runbook freshness points at update, and never contradicts D2
- WHEN the runbook copy differs by any byte from the packaged RUNBOOK.md
- THEN D3 is a finding naming `apriori update`; WHEN the copy is absent THEN D3 is not-applicable ("see D2") — absence is D2's finding alone

#### Scenario: DR-05 detected tools must keep their pointers
- WHEN a tool that `detectTools` finds has a missing rules file, a rules file without the `apriori/runbook.md` pointer, or (command-level tools) a missing command file
- THEN D4 reports a finding per tool naming the file and `apriori init`; a project with no known tool markers reports not-applicable

#### Scenario: DR-06 the TAP probe classifies every plumbing edge
- WHEN the test command (from `--test-cmd` or the config row) runs
- THEN D5 classifies: spawn error, signal kill, `Bail out!`, empty output, zero parsed TAP without a version/plan line (naming `--test-reporter=tap`), a TAP version or non-`1..0` plan with zero result lines ("truncated or malformed"), and a non-zero exit unexplained by parsed TAP failures — each a finding; parsed TAP whose failures explain any non-zero exit is ok (counts as info — test failures are NOT doctor findings), and an exactly-`1..0` plan with exit status 0 is ok ("empty suite") — a `1..0` plan with a NON-ZERO exit is the unexplained-exit finding, classified first

#### Scenario: DR-07 an explicit skip is not-applicable, a missing test command is a finding
- WHEN `--no-run` is given, or the effective id-pattern config is uncompilable
- THEN D5 reports not-applicable with the reason ("probe skipped (--no-run)" / "probe skipped (invalid id-pattern config)") — an explicit human skip is never a finding; WHEN instead NO test command is configured anywhere and no `--no-run` was given THEN D5 is a FINDING whose detail names the CONSEQUENCE (`apriori gate` cannot run C1, the binding check, at all) and whose fix names the `test-cmd` row in `apriori/process-config.md`, so the aggregate is `DOCTOR: <n> finding(s)` with exit 1 rather than `HEALTHY`; a CONFLICTING or unreadable `test-cmd` config stays the pre-existing config finding, whose detail is distinguishable from the missing-command one; the branch order is: invalid id-pattern → config problem → `--no-run` → missing command → run the probe

#### Scenario: DR-08 store health flags unbindable and ambiguous scenarios
- WHEN `apriori/specs/` contains scenarios without a leading default-pattern ID, or the same ID in more than one scenario
- THEN D6 reports findings listing them (detail names the default ID pattern); an empty store or zero scenario files is not-applicable ("normal for a new project"); a missing specs dir is not-applicable here ("see D2")

#### Scenario: DR-09 changes overview validates flow-states and surfaces pending gates
- WHEN an active change dir has no readable flow-state.md, or `parseFlowState` yields an empty `change`, or its `change` mismatches the dir name
- THEN D7 reports a finding naming the dir and the failed clause; healthy active changes are info lines; archived candidates (basenames matching `<YYYY-MM-DDThhmm>-<name>`, directories only) are read ONLY when they satisfy realpath containment under `apriori/changes/archive/` — an escaping symlinked entry is skipped with an info note, never read; contained not-DONE/ABANDONED archived changes are listed as info ("gate ④ possibly pending") — never a finding

#### Scenario: DR-10 output is machine-consumable in every class
- WHEN `apriori doctor --json` runs — HEALTHY, FINDINGS, or UNUSABLE (incl. the positional-arg usage error)
- THEN stdout parses as JSON shaped `{ result, findings, checks: [{id, status: "ok"|"finding"|"n/a", detail, fix?}], errors }`

#### Scenario: DR-11 doctor is read-only and rejects stray arguments
- WHEN doctor runs with `--no-run` against any project tree
- THEN no file is created, modified, or deleted; WHEN any positional argument is given THEN doctor exits 2 with usage (pure JSON under `--json`)

#### Scenario: DR-12 the Node floor is enforced testably
- WHEN the detected Node major version (injectable for tests) is below 22
- THEN D1 is a finding entry and doctor exits 2 (`result: "UNUSABLE"`), with D1 listed in the checks; at or above 22 → ok

diff --git a/CHANGELOG.md b/CHANGELOG.md

@@ -2,6 +2,40 @@
 
 All notable changes to `apriori-cli`. Versions follow semver; the stability promise: CLI surface & flags, `--json` shapes, the delta format and the flow-state schema only break in a major.
 
+## Unreleased · gate degrades the check it cannot run — `INCOMPLETE`, exit code 3
+
+**Exit-code semantics extended.** `apriori gate` gains a fourth outcome: `GATE: INCOMPLETE`
+with **exit code 3**, and `checks[].status` gains `skipped`. If your CI treats any non-zero
+exit as failure, an INCOMPLETE gate will now fail the job — that is the intended reading: a
+gate that could not run its binding check has not passed. The `--json` key set is unchanged
+(`{change, stage, checks, result, blocked, errors}` — still no `code` field); only the value
+domains of `result` and `checks[].status` grew, and `blocked` still counts blocked checks
+only, never skipped ones.
+
+**Why.** A project whose `apriori/process-config.md` lacked a `test-cmd` row got exit 2 from
+`apriori gate` before a single check ran — so C2 (tasks all checked) and C4 (ledger terminal)
+never executed either, though neither needs a test command. In a real brownfield project that
+meant the gate ran zero times across an entire change's life, while 45 unchecked tasks and
+zero consistency reviews sailed through. Now a missing test command skips C1 alone: the other
+six checks run and report real conclusions, and the aggregate says so. A *broken* test-command
+source (conflicting or unreadable config, an empty or whitespace-only `--test-cmd`, a
+non-string value through the API) stays exit 2 — broken is not absent. `--test-cmd` is now
+judged by PRESENCE, matching `--id-pattern`: an empty flag is an error, never a config
+fallback. A confirmed block still outranks a skip.
+
+**`apriori doctor` stops calling that project healthy.** D5 reported `n/a` when no test
+command was configured, and `n/a` is not a finding — so doctor printed `DOCTOR: HEALTHY` about
+a project whose gate was dead. It is now a finding naming the consequence, with the
+`process-config.md` fix. An explicit `--no-run` is still `n/a`: a deliberate skip is not a
+defect, and that branch now sorts ahead of the missing-command one.
+
+**Internals.** `lib/spec-runner.js` exports the change-projection builder plus a call-time
+resolver and two test-only seams, so gate's skipped-C1 path provably shares verify's one
+projection implementation rather than growing a second — a divergence there would have
+silently un-blocked C7.
+
+391 tests.
+

 The `--specs` byte goldens were captured on POSIX and compared byte-for-byte on every
